# Status

### Admin tools from the function audit: F-30, F-31, F-33, F-21, F-19, F-49 (Nov 24, branch `worktree-agent-af23136f86e585bfd`, NOT merged or deployed)
Code, tests and docs only; nothing was sent, charged or applied to production. Run locally (`wrangler pages dev`, local D1) and checked in Chromium (desktop 1280, phone 390, dark) with screenshots. `admin.js` 20261124g, `admin.css` 20261124d. Needs migration `0292_event_recheck_and_merge_undo.sql` (schema only: `events.checked_at`, `events.check_note`, `deleted_events.merge_undo`).
- **F-30 Merge duplicate events:** event editor > "Merge a duplicate" (this event stays). Suggests likely duplicates (same area, similar name or under 0.5 km), then shows a PREVIEW (`GET /api/admin/events/merge?keep=&duplicate=`, writes nothing): which fields differ, which the duplicate wins by default, what moves, warnings (pins far apart, dates do not overlap, both have organizers, hand-set Featured on the duplicate), and blockers. Default = the organizer-managed record's details win, as migration 0287 (name, city, price, hours, description, web page, venue, address, pin, owner photos, video clip and its approval), only where the duplicate has a value; untick any. `POST` (two taps): the survivor takes the ticked groups (photos are combined, the duplicate's first, at most 4); the duplicate's `event_submissions` (and edit history, Grok updates, home claim links) are pointed at the survivor; the duplicate row goes to `deleted_events` with a `merge_undo` note. REFUSED (409, with the reason) when the duplicate has any order (paid, refunded or held), any claim, a contest listed through it or an Event Partner record; every statement of the batch re-checks that, so an order arriving mid-merge stops it all. The survivor's paid placement is never touched. Admin log: action "merge", the survivor, "merged from <id> (name)", what moved and each changed field before and after. Undo (the toast, or `events/restore`) brings the duplicate back, puts the survivor's fields back (only the ones nobody edited since) and points the submissions home again. The video clip keeps playing because the photo route finds a clip by its own key (tested with the Holidays in the Village key). Tests: `tests/admin-event-merge.test.mjs` (the 0285/0286/0287 merges replayed).
- **F-31 Exports:** `GET /api/admin/export?type=` now also `orders` (refund status, buyer email, account email, plan, amount in dollars, Stripe payment and charge ids), `users`, `submissions` (optional `&status=`), `claims`, `reports`; Download CSV buttons on Orders, Submitted events, Event claims and Visitor reports (Users already had one). One shared `functions/_lib/csv.js` (`csvCell`: a leading `= + - @`, tab or CR gets a `'`; used by every export and the older users list). Tests: `tests/admin-export-search.test.mjs`.
- **F-33 Search:** events also match address, web link and id (an exact id first, also with the card's `e-` prefix); houses also match street; still 5 per group, one query per group.
- **F-21 No photo queue:** Events tab "No photo N" (upcoming events with no linked picture, no gallery and no owner photos; 589 of 1,062 on a local copy of the seed data), soonest first, with "Add photo" on each row that opens the editor scrolled to Photos. `GET /api/admin/event-queues`. Grok intake: NOT changed, it already accepts an image: the field is `image_url` (documented in `docs/monetization/grok-browser-pass.md`, validated by `checkImage`, tested in `tests/intake.test.mjs`).
- **F-19 Re-check a page:** event editor > "Check the event's page" > "Re-check this event's page" (`POST /api/admin/events/recheck`): polite fetch (`botfetch.js`, robots.txt first), shows whether the page still mentions the event name and its year and whether it uses cancelled, postponed or rescheduled words (a heads-up only), saved on the event (`checked_at`, `check_note`) and in the Admin log. A page that could not be read (blocked, robots, slow) keeps its note but is NOT stamped as checked. Nothing is downgraded, hidden or edited, and there is no schedule. Events list filter "Needs a look" > "Page not re-checked in 30 days (N)": upcoming confirmed events with a web page, never or over 30 days ago. `verifyEventPage` now also returns `seen`. Tests: `tests/admin-event-recheck.test.mjs`.
- **F-49 E2E workflow:** `.github/workflows/e2e.yml`, weekly (Monday 15:23 UTC) and manual, 25 minute limit, installs Playwright 1.63.0 and Chromium for the job only, local D1 and `wrangler pages dev` on 8791, runs the three scripts in order, uploads screenshots and logs. NOT verified on a GitHub runner. Run locally here: `signin-sheet.mjs` passed (second try; the first timed out on a cold server, so the workflow warms the pages first); `add-house-wizard.mjs` stopped at the address step and `profile-edit-delete.mjs` then had no home, and I could not tell network from script drift. The scripts print what they read but assert nothing, so green means "every screen appeared", not "every message was right". Make them assert and run the first manual run before trusting the weekly one.
- Tests: whole suite passes. Not done: bulk merge, merge of events that have orders (by design), an automatic or scheduled re-check.
### Function audit batch: F-43, F-13, F-14, F-26, F-27, F-10 (Nov 24, branch work, not deployed)
Code, tests and docs only; nothing was sent, charged or applied to production. Tests: whole suite passes (Stripe and Resend are stubbed in every new test).
- **F-43 Report or hide:** new `public/report.js` (same form and `POST /api/report` as the map; no new endpoint, `app.js` untouched). Contest page (`contest.html`): a Report link on every entry row in the gallery and the ballot (ref `h-<post id>`, the same ref a home uses), "Report this contest" (ref `c-<slug>`), "Hide for me" hides the row and shares the map's `socal-hidden` device list. Installer page (`functions/installer/[slug].js`): "Report or hide" in the footer (ref `i-<slug>`). Not added to `installer-manage.html` (the owner's own page).
- **F-13 Refund in Stripe:** Admin > Orders, `refund_stripe` on `POST /api/admin/orders/<id>` (paid, refund-due and held orders that have a payment intent; two-tap confirm). One `POST /v1/refunds` for the whole payment, idempotency key `refund-<session id>`; on success the order is recorded exactly like "Mark refunded" (placement off, note, buyer's "refund issued" mail once); on a Stripe error nothing changes and Stripe's message is shown. The Admin log records action `refund_stripe`. The later `charge.refunded` webhook finds it already refunded. `admin.js` 20261124d.
- **F-14 Placement ending mails:** templates `placement_ending` (3 days before) and `placement_ended` (the day after), `functions/_lib/placementmail.js`, `POST /api/placement-notices`, Worker job `placement-notices` (cron `25 18 * * *`, heartbeat job added), migration `0290_placement_end_notices.sql` (columns `ending_notice_at`, `ended_notice_at`; orders already ended are marked; heartbeat seed). Once per order and kind; paid and applied event or home orders only; never to a bounced, complained or deleted address (`guard: "deliverable"`). Kill switch `PLACEMENT_NOTICES=0`. A refund already had its mail. The Worker must be redeployed (`deploy-mail-cron.yml`) for the job to run.
- **F-26 Video mails:** templates `video_live` / `video_removed`, sent from Admin event video approve/remove and home video approve/remove (also contest entries, same handler) to the uploader, best effort. Only for an uploaded clip; approve mails only the first approval.
- **F-27 Weekly alerts cron:** root cause found: Cloudflare cron weekdays count 1 = Sunday, so `30 17 * * 4` fired on **Wednesday** (matches the Nov 18 heartbeat). Now `30 17 * * THU` in `wrangler.toml` and `CRONS`; a test forbids numeric weekdays. The heartbeat already existed (`alerts`, 192 h); a missed Thursday now shows as stale the next Friday. Needs the Worker redeploy; the first Thursday run is 8 days after the last one, right at the 192 h limit (a few minutes of "stale" is possible).
- **F-10 Ownership:** (a) `POST /api/me/email` (`start` / `confirm`, code to the new address, 4 an hour per account, 5 tries, refused for the same or an existing address, throwaway domains and an Admin acting-as); on success one batch moves `users.email`, `event_submissions.contact_email`, `posts.contact_email`, `installers.owner_email`, `event_claims.email`, `judges.email` and `contest_staff.email` (`functions/_lib/emailchange.js`, alias spellings of the old address follow). Account tab section "Change my email" in `profile.html`. Not moved: order receipts, email preferences, Admin-granted contest passes keyed `u:<email>`. (b) `POST /api/admin/transfer` (event, home, installer; the person needs an account; logged as action `transfer`), "Transfer to another account" box in the three Admin editors. Transferring an event leaves other people's approved claims in place and says how many.
### Data-safety fixes from the function audit (Nov 24, branch `worktree-agent-af0efd82b9a91e40f`, NOT merged or deployed)
Owner-approved items F-22/F-23, F-09, F-03, F-01/F-04 from `docs/audit-2026-11-24-functions.md`. Code, tests and docs only; no production data touched, no Stripe, no email.
- **Backup (F-22, F-23):** `scripts/lib/photo-keys.mjs` lists every PHOTOS key prefix (`p/ pending/ h/ v/ e/ ev/ avatars/ installer/ contestlogo/ sponsor/ sponsor-receipt/`) and the column behind each. `backup-photos.mjs` copies all of them and now exits 1 if a source could not be read; `verify-backup.mjs --photos <backup> --live-bucket <live>` fails when a file still in production is missing from the backup (rows already pointing at nothing are a warning); `backup.yml` runs it on Sundays after the copy. New `scripts/rehearse-restore.mjs` + `docs/restore-rehearsal.md` (owner runs it; reads R2 only). A test greps `functions/` and fails if the app writes a prefix that is not backed up. Not done: the rehearsal itself (needs the owner's Cloudflare login); the runbook still says so. Off-Cloudflare copy still manual.
- **Account deletion (F-09):** `deleteAccount` now deletes the events the person added (`u-` ids no one else manages), their owner photos and clip (after the batch), pending submissions, and flags live paid orders refund due (event id and `sub:<id>`), in the same batch. Events managed only through an approved claim, a takeover ("updated the existing listing" note) or with another manager are unlinked and stay. Home clips and hidden photos were already deleted (now tested). Tests: `tests/account-delete-events.test.mjs`.
- **Takeover revert (F-03):** migration `0288_event_edit_history.sql`; "This is my event" saves the full previous row (who, when, why) first and refuses the change if it cannot (503). `POST /api/admin/events/revert {id, action:"revert", unlink?}` restores the columns the takeover rewrites (not the paid placement, photos or video), marks the history row reverted, logged as "revert" in the Admin log. Admin event editor: "Organizer updates" section with "Revert the last organizer update" (and "Revert and remove their access"). `admin.js` 20261124c. Tests: `tests/event-takeover-revert.test.mjs`. Not added (as asked): the email-domain rule.
- **Homes (F-01, F-04):** migration `0289_post_review_note.sql` (`posts.review_note`). A normal home at an address another approved or pending home already has (same number, street, city; units ignored): the same person gets "You already added this home" (409), another person's is held for review with a note naming the other post. A Census "two possible matches" address is held with both candidates. Admin shows "Held for a look." on the waiting house. Not changed: the address-edit path in `me/homes/[id].js` (still no ambiguity check).
- **Production to fix by hand (not done here):** two approved homes at 25972 Limerick Ln, Lake Forest (`7af83399724347a2`, `4384e3d985f74580`). Check in Admin which is the real owner's; unpublish or delete the other through Admin (or a reviewed migration), and tell its poster. Not touched.
- Deploy note: migrations 0288 and 0289 apply when this reaches `main`; the code tolerates the brief window before they run (takeover answers "try again" until 0288 exists; the home note is skipped until 0289 exists).

### Paid pins are pulsing gold star badges (Nov 21)
Rey: a paid (featured) pin was only a slightly bigger green dot and hardly showed. Now every featured event and featured home is a gold star badge (34px, white ring, shadow) with two slow pulsing gold rings, drawn as page elements on top of the map (`drawFeatured`, `.fmark` in `styles.css`): always visible at every zoom, never merged into a cluster, tappable (opens the card), accessible label "Featured: <name>". ### Events: video clip upload (Nov 21)
Rey: events need a way for people to upload a photo or video. What existed: the event's organizer could add up to 4 photos (Your profile > Edit the event, once the event is on the map) and paste a YouTube or Vimeo link; there was no clip upload and the Add an event form offered neither. Now:
- **Video clip for events**, same rules as a home's (40 MB, about a minute, MP4/MOV/WebM, checked in the browser). Migration `0284_event_video_clip.sql` (events.video_file, video_ok). `PUT /api/me/events/<submission id>` uploads; the shared video box in the organizer's Edit form (Photos and video section) handles clip, link and Remove (`link` / `remove` actions). A link and a clip replace each other. The clip waits for Admin's approval; the organizer can watch it meanwhile (`/api/photo/ev/<eventId>/...`, Range supported).
- **Add an event "you're done" screen** (when the event is on the map): an optional video box (`#vslot`) for the event just added; photos stay in Your profile > Edit (the done screen's next-steps text says so).
- **Admin:** Events has a "Videos waiting" tab (count) and a "Video waiting" tag on the row; the event editor has a Video section (watch, Approve, Remove) via `POST /api/admin/event-video`; the Dashboard shows an "event video waiting for approval" card.
- **Public:** an approved clip shows as a "Watch the video" button on the event card (`vf`, detail-only like `vid`).
- Tests: `tests/event-clip.test.mjs` (2) plus updated form and version pins; 1206 pass. Versions: `app.js` 20261121c, `forms.js` / `videobox.js` 20261121a, `addflow.js` 20261121b, `admin.js` 20261121g.
Not done: photo upload on the Add an event form itself (the event does not exist yet to attach photos to); the Edit form is where photos live. Not checked: a real phone upload end to end (the test covers the API and the browser check covers Admin).

### Day / night now follows the Pacific sun, not the device setting (Nov 21)
Rey: night mode showed in daytime. Cause: the rule was "dark from 6 pm to 6 am by the device clock, OR whenever the device itself is set to dark", so a phone or computer in dark mode kept the site dark all day. Now every page (the head script in 32 pages, `functions/_lib/shell.js`, `public/app.js`, `public/chrome.js`, the offline page) uses one rule: dark between sunset and sunrise for Los Angeles on the Pacific clock (a day-length formula, within about ten minutes of the real times; tested against known Nov, Dec and Jun sunrise/sunset), light otherwise, whatever the device says. The sun/moon button still overrides until the next sunrise or sunset; an open page flips by itself (checked each minute). Test updated: `tests/seo-landing.test.mjs`. Versions: `app.js`, `chrome.js`, `addflow.js`, `appshell.js` 20261121.

Eight emulator runs (virtual Pixel 6 phone and Pixel C tablet, Android 14, debug build pointed at a local copy of the site). Checked and fine on Android: map with clusters and pins, event card (photo, tags, When/Cost/Hours/Where, Directions), Favorites tab and its "Plan your drive" card, account page ("Your profile", Sign in), Add page (An event / A lighting display), Help Center, dark mode; top and bottom bars clear the status and gesture bars after `adjustMarginsForEdgeToEdge: "force"`. Pricing redirects to the map inside the app (web-only, as intended). Not checked: the phone's event card and account pages (the driver's Escape key press closed the app on the phone, a test artifact, not something a user can do), real-device behaviour, the signed Play bundle, sign-in email flow, photo/video upload, share sheet. Next: Rey to confirm which Google account owns the Play Console (no Play email in socallightsapp@gmail.com), then the one-time upload key workflow and its four secrets, then "Android app" for the signed bundle and a Play closed test.

### Android test: run 7 findings (Nov 20)
Tablet (dark mode): the real app loads and the event card, Favorites tab and "Plan your drive in 3 steps" card all render correctly; no layout problems seen. Phone: map and bottom bar fine. The driver lost the phone's WebView because the script pressed Back (which closes the app at the root), and stalled on the account button (it navigates, so DevTools never answers); fixed: no Back press, 8 s timeout on every DevTools call.

### Android test: network fix (Nov 20)
Run 6: the driver attached on the phone but the app showed "address unreachable" for the local copy of the site (the virtual phone booted onto flaky 3G instead of Wi-Fi), and the tablet had no page. The app now reaches the local copy through `adb reverse` (localhost:8788) so the emulator's network no longer matters. Also: the driver's pricing/add/help steps need a real page URL once the app is loaded.

### Android test now drives the app (Nov 20)
`.github/scripts/android-drive.mjs` connects to the app's WebView in the emulator over plain DevTools (first try with Playwright failed: Android WebView has no browser contexts) and, after each step, saves a native screenshot (`d-01-...` to `d-09-...`): dismiss the welcome card, open an event card, close it, Favorites tab, Places tab, account button, then the Add, Pricing and Help pages, plus `drive-log.txt` listing which steps worked and any page errors. Run "Android test" and read those files.

### Android: status-bar fix confirmed in the emulator (Nov 20)
Third "Android test" run (after `adjustMarginsForEdgeToEdge: "force"`): on the virtual Pixel 6 and Pixel C the top bar (logo, search, buttons) now sits fully below the status bar and the bottom bar sits above the gesture bar; map, clusters, list and filters render with no app errors. Still to look at: the strip under the bottom bar is the window colour (light), check in dark mode; the list-drag screenshot did not move the sheet because the test swipe started on the bottom bar (test script only, not the app); other screens (event card, Add, Favorites, account menu) are not yet exercised. Next: build the signed Play bundle with "Android app" once the Play Console account is confirmed (Rey to say which Google account owns it; no Play email exists in socallightsapp@gmail.com).

### Android: first real screens, and the status-bar fix (Nov 20)
Second "Android test" run (local copy of the site) loaded the real app on the virtual Pixel 6 and Pixel C: map, clusters, list, filters and bottom bar all render; no app errors in the log. One real problem: Android drew the page under the status bar and the gesture bar (the top bar was cut off and the bottom bar touched the gesture line) because the wrapper had no inset handling. Fix: `app/capacitor.config.json` now sets `android.adjustMarginsForEdgeToEdge: "force"` (Android only; iOS untouched). Needs a new Android build to take effect, so re-run "Android test" to compare and "Android app" for the Play bundle.

### Android emulator: first run result and fix (Nov 20)
First "Android test" run: build, install and launch worked on the virtual Pixel 6 and Pixel C, but Cloudflare's bot check blocked the emulator (a GitHub datacenter address) so every screenshot was "Verify you are human". We do not bypass the bot check. The workflow now serves a local copy of the site on the test machine (same files, local database, migrations applied) and points the debug app at it (config changed only inside the CI run, nothing committed), so the real screens load in a real Android WebView. Note: this tests the site files and the Android wrapper, not Cloudflare itself. Google Play registration could not be confirmed: no Play email exists in socallightsapp@gmail.com (Gmail connected to Claude); Rey to check which Google account owns the Play Console.

### Android: emulator test workflow (Nov 20)
Rey has no Android device and has registered a Google Play developer account. Added `.github/workflows/android-test.yml` ("Android test", run by hand): builds a debug app, starts a virtual Pixel 6 phone and Pixel C tablet (Android 14) on GitHub, installs it, opens it, drags the list, taps the bottom bar, and saves screenshots plus an error log as the artifact `android-screens-phone` / `-tablet` (script: `.github/scripts/android-screens.sh`). Next: run it, read the screens, fix what is off, then re-run "Android app" for a fresh signed bundle and start the Play closed test (12 testers for 14 days is Google's rule for new personal accounts).

## 2026-11-18: The phone list follows the bar's real position (fix for the previous fix)
Rey's iPhone still showed the tab bar over the Filters row. Cause: the earlier fix measured `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` from script, which returned 0 in the app, so the list rose 8 px instead of about 24. `peekH()` now measures the tab bar itself (`#bnav` top) and ends the collapsed list 6 px above it, wherever the bar is. Tested by forcing the bar to 26 px (what the app computes): the gap to the Filters row is 6 px with and without that offset. Rey should confirm on the phone.

## 2026-11-18: The phone list rises with the bottom bar
After the bar was raised, it covered the Filters / Tonight / This weekend row of the collapsed list. The collapsed list height (`peekH()` in `public/app.js`) now adds the same raise as the bar (`max(10, safe area - 8) - 2` px), so the buttons row ends just above the bar. Measured in an iPhone-sized browser: Filters row bottom 588, bar top 594 (6 px clear). With the app's home-indicator inset both move up together; check it in the app.

- Fixed in #699: no 24px side gutter on phones on every page whose stylesheet sets its own body padding (the safe-area rule in `tokens.css` had id-weight specificity; now `:where()`), /contests scrolled itself down on load (panel ids matched the #live/#mine/#past fragment; now `p-*`), the "n of 15 judges" counter did not update while typing, the Report or hide dialog could not scroll on a short screen, the assistant button covered "Show N places" and the entry flow's Next.
- Left for the owner (judgment calls): the add flows show a field error and a banner error with slightly different wording at once; in the iPhone app the sign-in sheet says "Use your email instead" although Google is hidden; the visually hidden city list on the map page links "Feature your event" to /pricing inside the app (screen readers only); opening a `#e-...` link while the map is already open does not open the card (a fresh load does); one transient 502 on a tutorial poster image (`sponsors-invite.jpg`, fine on retry).

## 2026-11-16: Contest second pass (branch `claude/contest-second-pass`; audit H2, M7, M8, L9; H3 verified)
- H2 (HOLD): editing a contest to a name or organizer that newly sounds official (and the creator's email does not match the contest website) or that newly carries a review word keeps the contest live under its old name/text; the requested name, organizer, welcome text and rules wait in `contests.pending_edit` (migration 0276) and the review note, Admin gets the `contest_review` mail, and Admin's "Looks fine" applies them. The creator sees a "saved, rename needs a look" message on the dashboard. A matching organizer, an already-official name and an ordinary rename are never held. Create also holds review words (pending, note "review words").
- H3: verified already fixed in the payments PR (`cloneContest` refuses anything not live). Not redone. Still open: a clone does not count toward the 3-a-day creation cap.
- M7: server 400 "Use at most 8 categories." / "Invite at most 15 judges." (create, and categories on edit); wizard shows "n of 8 categories" and "n of 15 judges", disables Add at 8 and blocks Next above the limits.
- M8: contest entries need a photo; an entry from another city is kept but never auto-approved, the organizer's `entry_new` mail says so (new `note` variable) and the entrant is told; the entry page warns about another city. Duplicate address in a contest was already refused (payments M9); tests added. Not done: contest entries do not carry the "this is my home" statement or the unfound-address hold (organizer reviews them).
- L9: Turnstile on contest creation (wizard review screen, `verifyTurnstile` in `POST /api/contests`); one contest waiting for review per account (429); one `contest_review` mail per account per hour (also for held edits). Clone is unchanged.
- Tests: `tests/contest-second-pass.test.mjs` (8); three older tests adjusted for the new one-pending rule and the 8-category 400.

## 2026-11-16: Creation audit, part 2: accounts and email (branch `audit-creation-accounts`)
- M9 a sign-in code is tied to the browser that asked for it (short-lived cookie `scl_code_nonce`, only its hash is stored in `login_codes.nonce_hash`): a stranger's wrong guesses from another browser no longer use up the real person's five tries (they count only against 10 an hour per address and the 30 an hour per network cap), and a correct code typed on another device still works. Codes made before this behave as before. The limit of 4 codes per address per hour is unchanged (the App Store reviewer login uses it).
- L2 sign-in keeps the address as typed (`users.login_email`); receipts and the "we received your entry" mail go there; the account stays keyed by the stripped address. Other mail still uses the account address.
- L4 and L5 the two unsubscribe pages say which list they control and point at each other and at Your profile; changes are announced in a live region that is always on the page and the heading takes focus. `monthlyMayReceive` (`functions/_lib/newsletter.js`) is the one check the future monthly sender must pass (opt-in, reminder opt-out, hard bounce, complaint, deleted account).
- L8 `/api/address` answers only our own pages, needs a number and two words, and is limited to 60 lookups a minute per connection (an empty list when limited). L10 the profile picture is checked by its bytes and limited to 10 changes a day. L11 the "Remove my home" acknowledgment never repeats typed text.
- Migration `0275_account_login.sql` (two new columns, add-only). Tests: `tests/audit-creation-accounts.test.mjs` (10); `tests/appstore.test.mjs` keeps the code cookie between request and verify.

## 2026-11-16: Admin audit fixes, part 2 (event save rules, duplicates, deleted events, log values)
From the Admin and data audit 2 (H2, H4 code, M7, L4, L9). Stacked on part 1 (merge that first).
- **Admin event save enforces the data rules** (`adminEventRules` in `functions/_lib/events.js`, using `normalizeHours`, `freeFlagProblem`, the city list): hours are tidied automatically, Free with no price gets "Free", and a free/price disagreement, an http link, a pin too far from its city and a county that does not match the city's other events are refused with a plain sentence the form shows. A rule only applies to the fields the editor changed, so an older row that already breaks one can still be moved to another season or confirmed in bulk (and the bulk Undo keeps working).
- **Duplicates:** `functions/_lib/dupes.js` (`nearDuplicate`, `findNearDuplicate`): same start date and either near-identical names in one city (ordinals, "annual", "celebration" and the city name ignored) or the same venue or street with mostly matching names. The Grok intake now answers `duplicate` for those (also against the other rows of the same call), and Admin's Publish on a held, unpublished or rejected Grok row asks "Possible duplicate" first (Publish anyway sends `force`). Different shows at one venue the same day stay apart. The data clean-up of the 12 existing pairs is the data agent's migration.
- **Deleted events and undo:** migration `0268_deleted_events_and_log_detail.sql` adds `deleted_events` (a tombstone with the whole row, written by every Admin delete) and `admin_log.detail`. Single and bulk delete now offer **Undo** (`POST /api/admin/events/restore`, restores the whole row, lifts the mark, turns a "refund due" the delete caused back into paid). Saving an event in Admin on purpose clears its mark.
- **Migrations cannot silently revive an Admin-deleted event.** Migrations are plain SQL and cannot read the live table, so the gate does it: `d1-migrations.yml` lists `deleted_events` from the live database (best effort, never blocks) and `scripts/validate-migrations.mjs --deleted-file` fails a migration that adds or overwrites one of those ids unless it carries the comment `-- validate: revive <id>`. Limit: it only protects migrations pushed through that workflow, and only once migration 0268 is live and `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN` is set; the nightly sweeps and a local `db:migrate` are not checked.
- **Audit log shows before and after:** an event edit stores `{"field":[before,after]}` in `admin_log.detail` and the Activity log lists the changed fields under the entry.
- **L9 checked, no change:** the weekly email and the upcoming list only read `end_date >= today`.
- Tests: `tests/admin-event-rules.test.mjs`; `tests/intake.test.mjs` uses plainly different names where it sends several rows for one venue. Versions `admin.js?v=20261116z`, `grok.js?v=20261116c`.

## 2026-11-16: Payments audit, PR 1 of 3 (high findings H1 to H4; branch `audit/payments-high`)
- H1: a contest pass bought with no website now belongs to the buyer's email-domain organization (`d:covinaca.gov` for clerk@covinaca.gov; free-mail stays `u:<email>`), a pass running on that domain is found by a contest created with a blank or different website (unless the person bought for themselves), and /contest-pass has a "Contest website" box that says whose pass it will be.
- H2: "Run it again" works only for a live or announced contest; the copy keeps the source's review note.
- H3: every vote void/restore is written to the contest change log (voter code, category, home, reason; no email), the frozen results store how many votes were removed and the results page says so.
- H4: the vote review is paged (300 at a time, Show more, Show only flagged voters); totals and fraud flags are worked out in SQL over all votes.
- Tests: `tests/payments-audit-high.test.mjs`. No price, migration or public CSS/JS changed (no `?v=` bump needed).
- Owner decision pending (PR 3 lists it): organizers still see voter emails, because the Privacy page says so.

## 2026-11-15 (Grok update call; photo refresh weekly)
- Owner: "Build the Update Call." `POST /api/intake/updates` (same `GROK_INTAKE_KEY`, 8 rows a call, `dry_run`): Grok confirms `expected` events and corrects dates, hours, price or the photo link on events already on the map, from the event's own page. Same checks as the intake (real dates, not past, house-style hours never an estimate, free/price rule, `shows` date/time/place, evidence, `seen`, photo-link rules). Changes apply at once and keep their old values (table `intake_updates`, migration 0262); **Admin > From Grok > Updates** shows each with a one-click Undo. Held for the owner instead of applied: organizer-managed events (`u-` ids or any event with a submission), contest homes (`hcontest-`, `h-`), events with a paid placement, cancellations (`cancelled: true`, never applied automatically; Apply is not offered), a start more than 60 days away from an already confirmed one, a switched-off intake (`GROK_INTAKE_PUBLISH=0`), or the daily limit of 600. Held rows can be applied or dismissed in Admin; an undo or apply only works while the event still has the values it had. Names, venues, addresses, types and deletions cannot be changed through it.
- `GET /api/intake/work-order` also returns `events_to_confirm` (expected, soonest first, 40) and `events_without_photo` (40), leaving out organizer, contest and submitted events. Doc: `docs/monetization/grok-browser-pass.md` ("Events already on the map"); note for Grok in `docs/handoff/to-grok/`. Tests: `tests/intake-updates.test.mjs` (7).
- The scheduled **Monthly photo refresh** now runs **weekly** (Sundays 2:37 AM Pacific; renamed "[SCL] Weekly photo refresh"; its stored prompt still says "monthly" and its migration file suffix is YYYY-MM, which is harmless). Weekly upkeep, midweek upkeep and the photo refresh stay on until Grok has used the update call for about a week; the owner then decides whether to pause them.

## 2026-11-15 (From Grok: Publish anyway on rejected rows)
- Admin > From Grok > Rejected rows now have a **Publish anyway** button (owner override of the checks). Rejected rows used to keep only a few fields; they now keep everything Grok sent (hours, price, blurb, venue, address, page, evidence, photo link) so the owner can read and publish them. Publishing a rejected row still needs the basics (a name, a city from our list, a valid season and type, real dates), looks up the pin (city center when the address is not found, noted on the row), works out the county and the event id, refuses a name and date already on the map, and checks the photo link like the intake does. The event goes on the map as confirmed, tagged From Grok. Rows already stored before this change have fewer fields and may be refused with a message naming what is missing.

## 2026-11-15 (Grok intake: optional photo link)
- Grok's request (`docs/handoff/to-claude/2026-11-15-intake-photo-support.md`, PR 635) asked for photo support. Owner decided: **link the photos as we do now, do not copy them.** New optional row field `image_url` (migration 0261 adds `intake_events.image`): stored in `events.image` like every other event photo, so the card shows it from the event's site and credits "Photo: <site>" with a link; nothing is downloaded or stored. Checks: https, at most 500 characters, not a badge/placeholder/banner, and not a logo unless it is a city or government event (the sweeps' `imageProblem` rule, which lets a city keep its logo or seal), not a Facebook/Instagram/X/TikTok picture-server link (signed, expire in days), and one HEAD look that drops it only when it clearly is not a picture (HTML, 404/410, redirect to non-https, over 15 MB); a site that refuses the check keeps the link (the card falls back to its stock photo if the browser cannot load it). A bad photo never rejects the row: it is dropped and the answer's `image` field says why. Admin > From Grok shows the photo and has a one-click Remove photo (event stays). `docs/monetization/grok-browser-pass.md` updated. Rows per call stay 8 (tested under 50 outgoing calls with a photo check on every row).

## 2026-11-15 (menu animation restored, branch `claude/affectionate-faraday-0w156h`)
- Owner saw almost no animation in the Places / Favorites / Shop menu. Cause found by measuring in a browser: the later "sliding pill" rules in `public/styles.css` had switched off the label opening (width), the padding change and the icon pop, leaving only the pill and a color fade; and the reduced-motion rule (which Windows turns on when "Animation effects" is off) removed the rest. Now the pill, the opening label, the padding and the icon pop all run together, and under reduced motion the menu keeps its calm glide and label opening (only pops, spins and sweeping pages stay off). Then, from a video the owner liked, the pill got bouncier (springs LEAD k .2 c .66, TRAIL k .1 c .74: about a tenth of the distance of overshoot, settles in about half a second, squashes up to 13% while moving) and the icon pop is bubblier (scale .4 to 1.24 to .94 to 1). The pill test in `tests/frontend-logic.test.mjs` now expects that overshoot. Versions: `styles.css?v=20261115e`, `app.js?v=20261115f`.

## 2026-11-15 (Grok event intake, branch `claude/affectionate-faraday-0w156h`)
- Built what the owner approved: Grok sends rows it read in its own browser to `POST /api/intake/events` (Bearer `GROK_INTAKE_KEY`, at most 8 rows per call, `dry_run` supported). Every row is checked with the sweeps' rules (`scripts/event-rules.mjs` hours style and free/price rule, season and type lists, city list, blurb 20-180 characters, https event page, ZIP or cross streets, not ended, within 16 months, geocoded address within 30 km (45 for Los Angeles and San Diego), and the row must state the date, time and place with a quoted `evidence` and a `seen` day). Passing rows publish as **confirmed** events with an id starting `grok-`; the answer gives each row `published`, `held`, `duplicate` or `rejected` with reasons. A row never edits or deletes an existing event, and an event the owner took down is answered `duplicate` so Grok cannot add it back.
- Staging table `intake_events` (migration 0259). Publishing is **on by default** (owner asked for full automation): a row that passes every check goes on the map at once. The Pages secret `GROK_INTAKE_PUBLISH=0` is the kill switch (passing rows are then `held` for one-click Publish). Daily cap 60 published rows (the rest are held). Rejected rows are kept 30 days for the admin to read.
- Admin > Marketing > From Grok: tabs On the map / Held / Taken down / Rejected, with the page Grok read, what it said and the reason; one click Unpublish or Publish (a paid placement on the event is flagged refund due on unpublish and restored on republish). Event rows from Grok carry a "From Grok" tag in Admin > Events.
- `GET /api/intake/work-order` gives Grok the season categories that are thin today with the searches and places to look. Handoff for Grok: `docs/monetization/grok-browser-pass.md` (hand over the whole file). Tests: `tests/intake.test.mjs` (13).
- **Not live until the owner does these:** (1) create Pages secret `GROK_INTAKE_KEY` (16+ characters; the key goes only into Grok's secret store, never in chat), (2) give Grok the doc and schedule it every 2 days, (3) optional first run with `dry_run: true`. Browser pass fallback now targets the computer Rey-Home-Office (scheduled task renamed); it must be awake with Claude desktop and Chrome extension signed in. The migration applies when this merges.
- Caveats: Grok's cloud browser may still be blocked by some sites (datacenter addresses); the Tuesday/Friday Chrome browser pass stays the fallback. Rows are only as good as what Grok reads: the checks catch format, date, place and duplicates, not a page that is itself wrong.

## 2026-11-15 (verified bot: Web Bot Auth and the Cloudflare application)
- Owner: apply to Cloudflare's verified-bots program ("I like this idea"). Requirements read from Cloudflare's docs: honest identification by Web Bot Auth (Ed25519 signature, public key at `/.well-known/http-message-signatures-directory`) or IP validation (we have no dedicated IPs, so Web Bot Auth); obey robots.txt and keep rates reasonable; a disclosed purpose with public documentation; submit the BotBase form (Manage Account > Configurations > Bot Submission Form); no traffic minimum and no review time are stated. Verified status helps only on Cloudflare-fronted sites (our Cloudflare-challenge 403s), not Akamai ones, and only for fetches we sign; the sweeps' own browsing tools cannot sign, so the browser pass stays the route for what remains blocked.
- Built and tested: `functions/_lib/webbotauth.js` (signing + signed directory), `functions/.well-known/http-message-signatures-directory.js` (404 until the secret exists; `/.well-known/*` routed in `_routes.json`), `functions/_lib/robots.js` and `scripts/robots_ok.py` (robots.txt first for the server's page checks, `photo_pass.py`, `video_pass.py`), `functions/_lib/botfetch.js` (polite, signed fetch used by `submit-event`, the owner's edit and `verify-events`), `public/bot.html` (https://socallights.app/bot: purpose, user-agent, behavior, how to block, contact), `scripts/bot-keys.mjs` (`--make`, `--check`, `--crawltest`), `scripts/signed-fetch.mjs`. `docs/verified-bot.md` has the exact owner steps and the form values; `docs/sweep-methods.md` 5.3 has the conduct rules. Checked end to end on the dev server and against Cloudflare's test endpoint (crawltest.com answered 401 = format right, key not registered yet). Tests: `tests/robots.test.mjs`, `tests/web-bot-auth.test.mjs`.
- **Owner must do** (cannot be done from here): make the key, set the Pages secret `BOT_SIGNING_KEY`, redeploy, run `node scripts/bot-keys.mjs --check`, submit the BotBase form, then watch Submission history. Until the secret is set nothing is signed and the bot behaves as before (plus robots.txt courtesy).
- **Browser pass, where and when (owner asked):** Tuesdays and Fridays 10:23 AM Pacific on the owner's work computer (Claude desktop app open, Chrome open with the Claude extension signed in to the same account and organization). The Friday 2026-11-13 run found no connected browser, read nothing and sent a notification; next scheduled run Tuesday 2026-11-17. Written into `docs/browser-pass.md` and `docs/operating-guide.md`.

## 2026-11-15 (pick up everything per season: gap report, categories, venue watchlist)
- Owner: "We need to pick up everything per season. What do we need to add to our sweeps? ... Start, build it all the best way possible." Counted the live map (985 upcoming Christmas events): thin kinds were ice rinks 9, Polar Express and train rides 9, gardens 9, zoos 6, symphony/choir/Messiah concerts 12, A Christmas Carol 7, Las Posadas 2, Kwanzaa 3, holiday pop-up bars 2, New Year's Eve 20, and no search at all for Christmas tree farms. Same cause as the Ritz-Carlton miss: the sweeps found events city by city, so anything with its own events page that never reaches a city calendar was found by luck.
- Built: (1) `scripts/coverage-categories.mjs`: every season as a list of kinds of events with a working target, searches and where to look (Christmas 22 categories, the other five seasons 3 to 5 each); `node scripts/coverage-gaps.mjs --categories` prints them. (2) `scripts/coverage-gaps.mjs`: the gap report (each category against its target, groups ranked by upcoming events per city) and `--group N`, the **work order** for a group (cities and their counts, watchlist venues with no event on the map yet, thin categories with their searches). (3) `docs/watchlist.json`: 165 recurring venues (theme parks, zoos and aquariums, gardens, railroads, snow resorts, malls and outlets, fairgrounds, stadiums, performing-arts and symphony venues, historic sites, 31 hotels and resorts, churches, 19 tourism boards) each tied to the sweep group that reads it; 79 have a site address that answered 200, 7 are marked `blocked` (403, never bypassed), addresses that did not answer were left out. A starting list, grown by the sweeps. (4) Docs: new "Gap report, season categories and the venue watchlist" section in `docs/city-sweep.md`, `docs/coverage-cities.md`, `docs/operating-guide.md`. (5) The `[SCL] City sweep` scheduled task prompt now runs the work order first, reads watchlist venues and tourism-board guides, works the thin categories, adds discovered venues to the watchlist, and reports all of it. Tests: `tests/coverage-gaps.test.mjs`. First live run (2026-11-14): thin or low are Las Posadas/Kwanzaa/Three Kings 3 of 12, Nativity 5 of 12, Santa visits 70 of 80, concerts and choirs 34 of 60, home displays 154 of 250, holiday trains 11 of 15, menorah lightings 17 of 25, pop-up bars 10 of 15, Thanksgiving 26 of 30, theater 34 of 40; the thinnest groups per city are 2 (Valley), 11 (City of San Diego), 6 (Gateway Cities), 4 (Pomona Valley/East SGV). Categories match an event's name and venue only (not its description), because description text mentioning Santa or a historic place made everything look covered.
- **Correction to my earlier claim:** per resident, Los Angeles is the thinnest county (about 30 Christmas-season events per million people), then San Diego 41, San Bernardino 47, Riverside 54, Orange 58, Ventura 79, Santa Barbara 130. Ventura and Santa Barbara are well covered, so the fixed "thin days" groups (17 to 20: San Bernardino, Ventura, Santa Barbara) were aimed at the wrong places. The thin-day groups are now the four the gap report ranks thinnest. Population figures are rough 2020 numbers.
- Not possible: "roll last year's events forward" from our own data (the map only holds 2026 and 2027 events, none from 2025); the `expected` rows already do that job and `docs/confirmation-plan.md` confirms them. Targets in the categories file are working estimates, not facts about the region.
- **Order matters:** the sweep prompt already calls `node scripts/coverage-gaps.mjs`, which exists on `main` only after this is merged (the next sweep runs at 04:13, 12:13 and 20:13 Pacific).

## 2026-11-15 (tab pill: owner did not see the new animation)
- Owner: "I'm not getting the new animation" (after PR 623 was live: the site served the new `app.js`, so not a deploy or cache problem). Two causes fixed in `public/app.js` (`?v=20261115e`): (1) with the browser's **reduced-motion** setting on, the pill used to jump instantly. Some computers switch animations off (remote desktops, virtual machines, Windows with "Animation effects" off, iPhones with Reduce Motion); the earlier "hover media feature is false" result suggests the owner's Edge may be one. Now reduced motion gets a short, calm slide (no stretch, bounce or squash), never an instant jump. (2) `setTab` drew the list and map pins after marking the tab selected, which froze the pill's first frames (measured: the click handler blocks 30 to 55 ms for Places on a laptop, 200 to 250 ms with a 4x slower CPU, a typical phone). It now draws first, then marks the tab, so the pill starts on a free main thread. Test in `tests/frontend-logic.test.mjs`.
- Still true: on a slow phone the list's later pages (60 cards each) render in batches that can still cost frames; if the move is still choppy on the owner's device, the next step is to run the pill on the compositor (Web Animations) so it cannot be slowed by page work. Ask the owner for the device and browser, and whether Windows "Animation effects" or iOS "Reduce Motion" is on.

## 2026-11-15 (tab pill: slick, springy move)
- Owner: make the main navigation (Places | Favorites | Shop) a really slick and smooth animation. The sliding pill (`initSlidePill` in `public/app.js`, `?v=20261115c`; top tabs on desktop and the phone's bottom bar) no longer just chases its target. Its two edges are springs: the edge toward the destination launches first and fast, the trailing edge follows slowly, so the pill stretches toward the new tab and snaps in, squashes a few percent while moving, settles in about 300 ms with about 3 px of overshoot, and follows the tab while its label opens. Fixed 60 Hz physics steps, so it feels the same on 60 and 120 Hz screens. Reduced-motion users get an instant move. Measured in a browser: the pill lands within 0.2 px of the tab after a single move, five rapid clicks, a resize and with reduced motion. Test in `tests/frontend-logic.test.mjs`. Not seen by eye (a screenshot cannot show motion): the owner should try it on a phone and say if it should be bouncier or calmer (the two spring pairs `LEAD` and `TRAIL` are the knobs).

## 2026-11-14 (Ads: "Not in launch set" alerts)
- Owner pasted the Admin > Ads alerts: "Ad ct-a is not in the approved launch set. Do not run it." (also ct-b, ev-a, ev-b, in-a, in-b). Cause: the approved ad ids are `ct-a-meta`, `ev-a-meta`, `in-a-meta`... but Grok reported them as `ct-a`, `ev-a`, `in-a`; the check compares exact ids. The campaign spend and counts are Grok's real numbers under the short names.
- Fix: `canonicalAdId` in `functions/_lib/ads.js`; `POST /api/ads/report` stores a short name under the matching approved id (a held ad such as `ct-c` still raises the alert, and so does an unknown id; the same ad twice in one call is refused). Migration 0251 renames reports and log rows already stored under short names when the `-meta` id has no report yet, and DELETES an old short-name row when the `-meta` ad already has its own report that is as new or newer (the stale Nov 9 report of six paused copies with $0 spend: the live numbers are already under the full ids, so nothing live changes). Tests in `tests/ads.test.mjs`.
- Limit worth knowing: the site only sees ids, so it cannot tell whether the copy running in Meta is the approved copy; only that the ad names match. Tell Grok to use the exact ids from the brief (`GET /api/ads/brief`) from now on.

## 2026-11-14 (false "Deploy stalled" alerts)
- Owner: Admin > Errors showed "Deploy stalled alerts sent" 5 times, last 6 hours ago. Cause: Cloudflare Pages deployment `0ff1f32e` (production, commit "iOS build workflow: use the APPSTORE_* secret names (#611)", created 2026-11-14 01:12 UTC) never closed: its record has stayed on "deploy: active" for 14+ hours although every later deployment, production and preview, finished in about 1.5 minutes. The rule "active for 8+ minutes = stalled" flagged it again and the owner was emailed every 3 hours.
- Fix: `functions/_lib/deploys.js` ignores an "active" record when a LATER deployment has finished its deploy stage (the queue is moving, so the old record blocks nothing). A newest-or-only stuck deployment, or one with only older finished ones behind it, still counts. Tests in `tests/monitoring.test.mjs`. The stale record itself is still in Cloudflare (Workers & Pages > socal-lights > Deployments); the owner can delete it there, but it no longer raises alarms. "Behind main" and "failed production build" rules are unchanged.
- The Errors card counts alerts from the last 7 days, so the 5 will fall out of it by themselves.

Still on GitHub: digest, nightly backup, uptime check (Better Stack also watches), validate-migrations. Caveat: "success" in Cloudflare means the Worker did not crash, not that each job said ok; the heartbeat banner and `/api/health` now flag a quiet or failed job within the hour. `docs/runbooks/monitoring.md` has the how-to-recover note and how to go back.

## 2026-11-14 (organizer dashboard: "This isn't your contest" in the Admin view)
- Owner: after the owner lookup the dashboard opened as the right person (cparade@covinaca.gov) but said "This isn't your contest". Cause: the page reads the contest from its own address (`location.pathname.split("/")[2]`), and the Admin view lives at `/admin/as`, so the contest it asked for was "as". The server side was fine (checked: detail, manage, votes, judges, stats, changes all answer 200 and `isOwner: true` for the contest's owner).
- Fix: `functions/admin/as.js` passes the real page path (`to`) to the shim, and `public/admin/as-shim.js` rewrites the address bar to it (`history.replaceState`) before the page's own scripts run. A reload then opens the plain page (the owner's sign-in); use the Admin link again to come back in. Same fix covers the installer manage page and any page that reads its own address. Test in `tests/admin-as.test.mjs`. Not yet tried on the live site.

- Owner: "Organizer dashboard (as the organizer)" opened the page with the red Admin bar but the page said "Organizer sign-in", and the bar named the page path instead of a person. Cause: the contest and installer links carry no `?as=`, and the page's own "who am I" call (`/api/auth/me`) has no contest or installer in its path, so the act-as route could not tell who to be (404, so the page thought nobody was signed in). ## 2026-11-14 (sites behind bot protection: Holidays in the Pines)
- Owner: "Holidays in the Pines" (Crestline, visitcrestline.org) showed the note "event page returned HTTP 403" although its page opens fine in a browser. Cause: the site is behind Cloudflare's "Just a moment..." challenge (`cf-mitigated: challenge`); it answers every automated visitor with 403, with our honest bot name or without. We do not disguise the check or solve challenges (`docs/blocked-sites.md`).
- `verifyEventPage` now recognises a challenge (403/429/503 with `cf-mitigated: challenge`, Sucuri or Akamai headers), makes one try (no retry), and reports `blocked` with the note "the site's bot protection blocked our automatic check (the page itself is probably fine: open the link yourself, then confirm the dates)". `verify-events` asks such a site again after 3 days, not 6 hours.
- New Admin action: published events in Admin > Submitted events whose event is still Expected get a **Confirm dates** button (`action: "confirm"` in `functions/api/admin/submissions/[id].js`; submission `verified=1`, note "confirmed by the site owner after checking the page"). The list API returns `event_status`. `admin.js` `?v=20261114b`.
- Not automatic: a blocked site cannot be confirmed by the job. Open question for the owner: auto-confirm when the organizer's contact email is on the same domain as the event page (needs a decision on how much to trust an unverified email).

- Owner: a submitted event stayed "expected" after a failed page check and Admin showed no way to confirm it (Admin > Submitted events only has Unpublish; the "Dates are" choice is in Admin > Events > the event > Edit), and hundreds of these must not need a hand.
- New job `verify-events` on the Cloudflare clock (`13,43 * * * *`): `POST /api/verify-events` (key-protected, `functions/api/verify-events.js`) takes up to 6 published, still-expected, upcoming events that a person submitted and that have a web page, runs the same check as the submit form (`verifyEventPage`: page loads, mentions the name and the year) and sets the ones that pass to confirmed (submission `verified=1`, note "confirmed automatically: ..."). A page that still fails is noted and asked again after 6 hours until the event ends. Never downgrades; never touches sweep-found events (they follow `docs/confirmation-plan.md`). - Owner got a submission (Night in Bethlehem, Gateway Church of the Nazarene, Murrieta) with the note "event page could not be loaded" and the label "Expected from past years". Not from a sweep: `submit-event.js` marks a user's event "expected" whenever the page check does not pass. The church site (GoDaddy) answered slowly or dropped the connection (2 of 3 test loads reset after about 10 s; the third took 0.6 s and mentioned the name and 2026), and the check's 6 s limit gave up.

## 2026-11-11 (home page speed, audit SEO-01, branch fix-perf, not merged)
- Measured on a simulated slow phone (400 ms latency, 1.6 Mbps, 4x CPU, Playwright + CDP, 4 runs, local copy with the resizer stubbed): list on screen 4.1 s -> 2.8 s; with the map held back (what a phone with a real GPU sees) first card photo 3.5 s -> 3.3 s and transfer 522 KB -> 442 KB in 9 s. Live before (anonymous GET): first paint 2.2 s, list 3.8 s, LCP 6.6 s, TBT 4.2 s, 2.2 MB. The lab has no GPU, so the software-drawn map keeps the processor busy for seconds and shows up in LCP/TBT; those two numbers are only comparable between runs, not to a real phone.
- What changed: (1) the 208 KB map library is no longer preloaded; app.js starts it after the first photos have loaded (or at the first touch/key), see `mapWhenCalm`; (2) `/api/events?head=1` is a small first-screen share of the list (about 8 KB instead of 110 KB: the events of the default season up to the 80th, plus paid top placements, see `headOf`); index.html asks for it first and for the whole list right after, the page draws from the head and swaps in the whole list (`takeFull`, counts hidden meanwhile); a link with a #hash waits for the whole list as before; (3) the Plus Jakarta Sans font is served from `/fonts` on the home page (no font host before first paint; other pages unchanged); (4) the assistant button's script loads when the page is idle (the button itself is unchanged); (5) header badge is a 6 KB copy; stock thumbnails go through the image resizer like our other photos; (6) the list is not redrawn when the first page of cards stays the same.
- Tests: `tests/home-speed.test.mjs` (head contents, asset budget, no map/chat/font-host on the critical path).
- Owner decision: the map now appears about 0.7 s later on a slow phone (it was competing with the list). Say if you prefer the map first.

## 2026-11-11 (front-end polish, branch `fix-frontend`; audit MAP-05..19 and A11-01..21)
- **One shared stylesheet** `public/tokens.css` (A11-08): every color, font and shadow is defined once (one Christmas red `#c9313b`, `--surface`/`--tile`, `--ice`, `--line2`, new `--field` for text-field borders, `--navhi`, `--season-ink`); the map, add flows, server pages (shell.js), Help and the 404 all link it before their own CSS. `tests/css-tokens.test.mjs` fails on any `var(--x)` that is not defined there (or in the same file) and on a re-declared palette. This fixes A11-01 (Remove-my-home white input in night mode) and A11-02/CON-13 (Founder city tag contrast) at the root. Removed the unused `flow.css`.
- **One header** (A11-07): 72 px everywhere (the add-flow header was 88), same wordmark and the same Map link (`.maplink`, now in the HTML of every header instead of added late by footer.js) on static pages, server pages, the 404 and the app-frame top bar (Pricing, Help, Tutorials, Terms, Privacy, Profile...). Not unified: the app-frame pages keep their side menu and title in the bar (the wordmark is in the side menu), the add wizards keep Save & exit, contest-enter keeps Back, offline.html has no header.
- Map: Sort and the count stay above the tab bar on small phones (peek 232); controls, Route these and the sheet handle are 44 px (MAP-05/06); sponsor strip shows one whole sponsor at a time (name and phone), 44 px links, pause button, still labelled Sponsor (MAP-07); season and search wording are in the first HTML (`applyHomeMeta`, MAP-09/10); one count "N places in view" (MAP-11); season menu dims the page (MAP-14); Favorites Start has My location, First stop and a typed city (MAP-16); "Tick a stop" (MAP-17); whole visited row taps (MAP-19).
- Photos (MAP-18): `public/photoguess.js`. A non-city event whose photo is named like a logo/seal/badge, is a wide or tall strip, or a small square shows the stock picture; blurry or tiny photos fall back for everyone. City/government events (name, venue or organizer text such as "City of", "Parks and Recreation", or a link or picture on *.gov, *.ca.us, cityof*, ci.*) keep their logo. Server event and city pages drop logo-named pictures for non-city events. A data-side sweep is a separate batch.

Skipped by the owner: MAP-08, A11-09 (display/house wording).

Audit items SEO-02 to SEO-12, MAP-12, MAP-13, MAP-15, ADM-01 to ADM-11. Full rules and trade-offs for the search items: `docs/seo-indexing.md`. Tests: `tests/seo-admin.test.mjs`.
- **Search.** Expected event pages can be indexed when they have a source link and start within 180 days (never Event data); `/e` pages, city pages, Help and Past redirect (301) capital-letter and trailing-slash variants; sitemap has `lastmod` from each event's `updated_at`; event share image goes through `/cdn-cgi/image` with size tags and `twitter:image` (also contest pages and every `page()` page); event titles are cut at word boundaries; Pricing/Contests/Tutorials have canonical and share tags; home has Organization data and city pages an ItemList; `/past` is noindex while empty; `robots.txt` no longer blocks `/c/`; city descriptions name the top three events; HSTS plus a REPORT-ONLY CSP (not enforced, no collector: read the browser console).

- **Event page.** Favorite (same `socal-favs` list as the map), Add to calendar (`/e/<id>.ics`), Directions; breadcrumb separator no longer starts a wrapped line.
- **Admin.** Dashboard: "map events" and the Events badge now count what the public map shows (was every stored row: 1,907 locally against 1,106 on the map), with confirmed and expected; Data health card (no photo, expected and date passed, no link, not updated in 14+ days) whose rows open the Events tab already filtered (the same predicates, `public/admin/health.js`); revenue strip for 30 days by product (Orders tab too; `GET /api/admin/revenue`); "Errors, last 7 days" and "Last run of each job". Events tab: season "All", status, county and "Needs a look" filters, sort, bulk select (set confirmed/expected and move season with Undo, delete with a second tap and no undo). Submitted events: bulk publish/decline (decline can be undone) and sort. Houses: sort. Tabs regrouped Review, Content, Money, Marketing, System (all 21 tabs kept, deep links like `#contests` unchanged; new `#passes` shortcut opens Contests on its passes view). Browser `confirm()`/`prompt()` replaced by tap-again or in-page windows (9 places). Phones: long cards fold to a title line and show 20 at a time.

## 2026-11-11 (add flows, the rest: branch fix-add-rest, PR on top of fix-add-flows, not merged)
- From the same audit: ADD-11, 13, 16, 17, 18, 19, 20, 22, 23. ADD-12 (Step n of N, aria values) and ADD-14 (grey Optional) were already in fix-add-flows. ADD-08, ADD-15, ADD-21 left alone on purpose.
- Photos (ADD-11): remove target and the recheck after each shrink were already 44 px / done; added the sentence that iPhone HEIC photos are converted, and a specific message when a computer browser cannot open a HEIC file.
- Review (ADD-13): every answer is listed (event: season, type, hours, description, street; home: description, visit tips, installer or contact details). The whole row is the tap target (the Edit button stretches over it); an empty optional answer reads "Not added".
- Counter (ADD-16): "n/200" under the event description and "n/1200" under the home description, with "at least 20 / 10 characters" until reached.
- I-am-human check (ADD-17): CHOSE TO VERIFY ON THE SERVER. New events and new homes now send the Turnstile token and `functions/api/submit-event.js` and `submit.js` check it with `verifyTurnstile` (contest entries are not asked: that page has no widget). Reason: sign-in alone (Google especially) does not stop a script that holds a session. Same fail-open rule as every other form: no `TURNSTILE_SECRET` means allowed (local dev), `TURNSTILE_REQUIRED=1` previews refuse. Risk: a visitor whose browser blocks challenges.cloudflare.com cannot post; the form says to turn off the content blocker. To undo, remove the two `verifyTurnstile` lines and `ts.need()` in the pages.
- Signed-out (ADD-18): the /add tiles say "You'll sign in first (free, no password)"; the sign-in gate pages show the theme and account buttons.
- Header (ADD-19): Save & exit appears once something is typed or chosen (before that, a plain Exit); a "Need help?" link (new tab, "Help" on phones) goes to the matching Help article. At 480 px and below the wordmark hides so the header does not overflow.
- Declined email (ADD-20): `functions/_lib/declined.js` saves the declined home's answers as a draft on the account (not for contest entries, not past 10 drafts) and the mail links to `/add-house?draft=<id>`, which opens on the review screen; otherwise the blank form as before. Photos are not part of a draft, the mail says so. The sign-in gate now keeps `?draft=` through sign-in.
- Wide screens (ADD-22): from 900 px the progress line, Back and Next sit under the form instead of fixed to the bottom. Skip link and main landmark on the add pages and gates (ADD-23).
- Tests: `tests/add-rest.test.mjs`. Browser check done locally (phone, 360, desktop) with the challenge script stubbed.

## 2026-11-11 (add flows and sign-in fixes, branch fix-add-flows, PR open, not merged)
- From the owner-reviewed audit `docs/audit/2026-11-11-add-flows-and-signin.md` (branch audit-add): ADD-01 to ADD-07, ADD-09, ADD-10 (ADD-08 wording left alone on purpose).
- Photos (ADD-01): kept in IndexedDB on the device with the draft (`public/drafts-local.js`), so they survive reload, Save & exit and a sign-in round trip; real thumbnails and a Remove button on the review screen. They are NOT uploaded until the form is sent (no R2 cost, no clean-up job); they stay on the device where they were added.
- Edit sheet (ADD-02): the profile's Edit now has season, photos (add, remove, make cover, shrunk on the phone first) and address (re-geocoded; events also season, type, city). Same checks as a new submission (`functions/_lib/moderation.js` is the shared "needs a look" rule): spam words put an edited home back in review and say so on screen. Events: spam words are refused as for a new event; a changed web page, name or first day is re-checked (Confirmed or Expected).
- Receipts (ADD-03): `home_live`, `home_received`, `event_live`, `event_received`, `contest_live_now`, `contest_received` templates, sent by `functions/_lib/receipt.js` (service mail: unsubscribed and removed people still get them; skipped only for hard bounces, complaints, invalid addresses and deleted accounts; 20 a day per address). Done screens say what happens next and link to Your profile. Contest ENTRIES get no receipt. Resend free plan is 100 a day: each submission is now one more mail.
- Duplicate event (ADD-04): server answers mine / not mine / featurable; one screen per real state; Feature only for your own live, featurable event.
- Limits (ADD-07): `functions/_lib/submitlimit.js`. Per account (homes 5 a day, events 5 an hour and 8 a day) is the real limit; the per-connection cap went from 5 to 30 an hour so shared Wi-Fi is not blocked. Every 429 says which limit and when it resets, with Retry-After and the contact address. Quote emails: per-connection 10 to 30 a day (account still 5). Weekly-alert per-connection stays 20. Network failures show a plain sentence and a Try again button.
- Sign-in sheet (ADD-05): focus trap, inert page behind, Escape and focus return, role=alert errors tied by aria-describedby, email and code checked before any request. Wizards (ADD-06): focus goes to the field the message is about (`bad(msg, field)` in step checks), every earlier step is re-checked before sending.
- Drafts (ADD-09): keyed by account, removed at sign-out and account deletion, "Start over" (tap again) and the saved time shown. Profile (ADD-10): opens on what was just posted, Save & exit lands on Drafts; delete names the item and has an 8 second Undo (the request is sent after the window: the database has no soft delete).
- Browser checks: `tests/e2e/*.mjs` (by hand, local dev only). Server tests: `tests/add-flows.test.mjs`, `tests/submit.test.mjs`.

## 2026-11-12 (deploy watch on the Cloudflare clock)
- The Admin banner "Deploy watch: last ran 6.3 hours ago" was GitHub's scheduler again. The deploy watch now runs from the Cloudflare Worker every 10 minutes (`/api/deploy-check`, new): it reads main's latest commit from GitHub's public API, the site checks Cloudflare Pages (stuck deployment, failed production build, production behind main) and emails the owner at most every 3 hours while stalled (new "Deploy stalled alert" email), then reports to the `deploy-watch` heartbeat. The GitHub workflow stays as a safety net until stage 2. The usage and deploy alert emails now record "sent" only after the email really went out, so a failed send is retried at the next check.

## 2026-11-12 (list springs back when pulled down)
- On a phone, pulling the list down past its resting spot stretches it (growing resistance, up to 70 px) and it springs back with a small bounce when let go (not with reduced motion). Tested with a mouse drag: stretched to 47 px past rest, released, overshot 12 px up and settled exactly at rest.

## 2026-11-12 (live location on the map)
- The locate button now follows you: a blue dot with an accuracy ring and a direction arrow moves with your phone (GPS, high accuracy); the map follows until you drag it (the dot keeps moving), tap again to follow again, tap once more to turn it off. Distances and the nearest sort refresh every 30 seconds. Nothing is sent to the server. Updates pause while the page is in the background (an iOS rule). Tested with a simulated moving phone: dot stays centred while following, moves on its own after a drag, re-locks on tap, removed when turned off.

## 2026-11-12 (list bounce on load, phone)

Endpoint `/api/contests/<slug>/logo` (GET public while live, POST/DELETE organizer team), file in R2 `contestlogo/<contest id>/<ms>.<ext>`, column `contests.logo` (migration 0224). Deleting a contest deletes the file.
- **No logo uploaded:** an icon is drawn from the contest title (`public/contesticon.js`: first letters of the first two words, color picked from the name, so each contest looks different and stays the same). Same function in the browser and on the server.
- Founder city badge: bigger star with a gap before the text.
- Tests: `tests/contest-logo.test.mjs`. Not done: the logo does not show on the `/contests` list cards or on past-results pages.

## Contest pricing: organizers pay from the first contest (2026-11-09)
Branch `contest-pricing-2026-11`, PR to `main`, go-live 2026-11-09 (owner decision). Owner decision after meeting the City of Eastvale (who recommended charging). Replaces the 2026-11-07 rule (1 free contest per 12 months; $50 / $75 / $100).
- **Prices** (`functions/_lib/passes.js`, still the only place a price is set): Single contest `pass_1` $149 (cap 1), up to 5 `pass_5` $299, up to 20 `pass_20` $499, more than 20 contact us. `FREE_CONTESTS = 0`. Upgrades cost the difference ($150, $200, $350). `pass_10` ($75, never sold as far as is known) is no longer in `CONTEST_PASSES` (not offered, not buyable) and lives in `LEGACY_PASSES` so an order already paid still applies, counts and can be revoked. Comps ("Founding partner: free through <date>") work as before and a comp organizer never sees a price; payment never affects results, votes, judges or ranking; entering, voting, the map, search and routes stay free.
- **Window:** 12 months from the day the pass is bought (a pass bought before any contest starts the window; `contest_passes.window_start` seeds it) or from the first contest going live, whichever comes first. Webhook check (`quoteProblem`) accepts a pass quoted with no window running unless its quoted window already ended.
- **Organizations that used their free contest under the old rule:** contests that went live before `PAID_FROM` are never counted and never need a pass, and stay live untouched; the organization is treated as new (its next contest needs a pass, and the pass starts a new window). `PAID_FROM` is `2026-11-09` (go-live day, Pacific).
- **Pages and copy:** Pricing (free card removed, three passes), contest pass page, wizard review (the picker is shown for a first contest), Add page tile (price from `/api/config`, no "Free"), Terms (Contest organizer passes), Support sheet, receipt email, chat assistant (`chatkb.js`, `chatcards.js`, `docs/ai-assistant-kb.md`, sample QA, `chatkb-data.js` regenerated; old pricing lines in tutorial captions are filtered out of the assistant's knowledge until the videos are re-recorded). Help articles: `migrations/0213_help_contest_pricing.sql` (generated by `scripts/help/updates-contest-pricing.mjs`, only edits articles still `updated_by = 'seed'`; paste text in `docs/help-updates.md`). Asset `?v=` bumped for `app.js`, `admin.js`, `contest-passes.js`.
- **Marketing:** `docs/marketing/contest-pricing-impact.md` lists every ad, post, picture and tutorial video that still says "free" for contests, with a proposed replacement. Ads, posts, pictures and videos were NOT edited. The Grok email playbook and outreach guide were corrected.
- **In the store apps (fixed):** no price and no payment are shown in the app (docs/app-store.md: Apple and Google want in-app purchase for digital goods, so the apps leave payments out; the codebase has no signed-in handoff, sessions are per cookie jar). The wizard's pass picker is replaced there by `Continue on socallights.app` (opens `/contest-pass?site=<contest website>` in the system browser through the Capacitor Browser plugin, else a new window), then `I have my pass`, which re-checks the entitlement and creates the contest from the draft kept in the app. The pass page keeps `?site=` through Stripe so the pass goes to the same organization; the organizer signs in there with the same email. Server checkout from the app still answers 403. Not tested on a device.
- **Tests:** `tests/contest-passes.test.mjs` rewritten (free count 0, `pass_1` price, upgrade differences, comp unaffected, `pass_10` retired but honored, legacy organizations, window seeded by a pass); the other contest tests use `compEveryone` (`tests/_pay.mjs`). Full suite passes.

Owner decision after meeting the City of Eastvale: listing an event stays free; per-event prices unchanged; new **Event Partner** plan for cities, chambers, nonprofits and event producers, $149 per season, up to 10 events, each with the Featured pin (level 1) for its run window plus an "Event Partner" badge. Numbers and rules: `docs/monetization/finance-pricing.md` section 2f.

- **Pages and API:** `public/partner.html` (buy, events used x of 10, "Use the plan" per event), `public/partner-copy.js`, Pricing card in the Event organizers panel, link from `/feature`; `/api/partner/{entitlement,checkout,apply}`; webhook handles `target: event_partner`; receipt email template `event_partner_paid`; Admin > Event Partners (`public/admin/partner.js`, `functions/api/admin/partner.js`: organizations used x of cap, comp grant form, end a comp, free a slot) and a card type in Admin > Orders. Badge: `ep` / `epv` on events (`rowToEvent`), shown in the event details in `public/app.js` (`?v=20261110ep`).
- **Founder cities:** migration `0211_event_partner.sql` grants comps to `d:eastvaleca.gov` and `d:covinaca.gov` (10 events per season, stable ids, idempotent) free **through 2026-12-31 only** (this year; after that they see the normal price). A separate branch handles their Founder city badge and the contest comp.
- **Help and assistant:** migration `0212_help_event_partner.sql` (article `event-partner-plan`), `docs/ai-assistant-kb.md`, `chatkb.js` price facts; chat KB regenerated.
- **Tests:** `tests/event-partner.test.mjs` (20): price, cap of 10, window rules, comps, refund, dispute, replay, Admin.

Warnings: over $280 / over $300, campaign over budget, ad not in launch set, active after Dec 8, active past its campaign end, disapproved, no report in 48 hours, spend decreased.
