## Cloudflare usage is plan-aware: Workers Paid (2026-11-08)
The owner upgraded to Workers Paid ($5/month) after the free-tier D1 rows-read outage; the panel, digest and check had still compared against free daily limits (false "limit reached"). Branch work, PR to `main` (not merged by the agent).
- **Plan setting.** `CLOUDFLARE_PLAN` (`wrangler.toml` `[vars]`, code default `paid`; `"free"` restores the old behaviour, tested). `CLOUDFLARE_BILLING_DAY` (1-28, default 1, **unverified**: the owner must check the real cycle day in Billing). Resend stays free-plan (`RESEND_PLAN`).
- **Numbers (`functions/_lib/cflimits.js`, sources and fetch date 2026-11-08 in the header).** Paid: D1 rows read 25 billion/month then $0.001 per million; rows written 50 million/month then $1.00 per million; D1 storage 5 GB then $0.75 per GB-month; requests 10 million/month then $0.30 per million. R2 unchanged on both plans (10 GB, 1M Class A, 10M Class B; $0.015/GB-month, $4.50 and $0.36 per million). Not tracked: Workers CPU-ms allowance (30 million/month, $0.02 per million).
- **Query.** `buildQuery`/`parseAnalytics` take the billing-month start (UTC) and return month sums alongside today and the 7-day trend; the D1 cache is keyed by day and period start.
- **Panel.** Header "Plan: Workers Paid ($5/month)", billing month start, estimated extra charges so far; cards read "X of Y included this month", show today, a cost estimate once past the included amount, and the tag "Extra charges start" at 100% (thresholds still 70/90/100%). Free plan shows the daily limits as before. `admin.js` `?v=20261111d`.
- **Alerts.** Digest section "Cloudflare Workers Paid usage" with plan line and estimate, subject `USAGE` (free plan or a logged quota error: `LIMIT`); `POST /api/usage-check` also answers `plan`, `estimatedOverageUsd`, `hardStop`; `usage-issue.sh` issue is now "Cloudflare usage limit reached or near" (an open issue with the old free-plan title is reused and renamed) with paid wording.
- **Tests.** `tests/cf-usage-paid.test.mjs` (mocked GraphQL, no real API); `tests/cf-usage.test.mjs` pins `CLOUDFLARE_PLAN: "free"`. `tests/chat.test.mjs` "generated knowledge base file is current" was already failing before this change (stale `functions/_lib/chatkb-data.js`; fix: `node scripts/build-chat-kb.mjs`), left alone.
- **Owner to verify in Cloudflare dashboard:** Billing shows Workers Paid and the billing cycle day (set `CLOUDFLARE_BILLING_DAY`); D1 and Workers usage on the invoice roughly match the panel; the D1 rows-read count after the outage (inefficient queries still cost money past 25 billion, but at $0.001 per million); whether Pages Functions requests bill under Workers (the pricing page says so). Set a Billing notification/budget alert in Cloudflare for a safety net.

## 2026-11-10 (AI assistant tone and format)
- Assistant prompt (`RULES` in `chatkb.js`) now asks for a warm, upbeat but professional tone, one short lead-in then numbered steps and `- ` bullets (plain text, no bold or headings), under about 1000 characters. Output filters unchanged. New KB chunks: menus (three dots vs account circle), Favorites, Remove my home, Promote your event, Weekly lights email. "My night" and "Open route" are rewritten to "Favorites" and "Route these" in anything shown to the model; privacy.html now says Favorites. Fixed the orphaned-photos bug: `submit.js` now deletes uploaded photos when the per-account daily limit refuses a home (the test is no longer TODO). Refund questions now get the published refund rules first (new chunk "Refund rules for paid placements and passes"; `RULES` says to give everything the sources say before offering the email). Draft-saving wording says Google or an emailed code; dashboard lists invite judges. **Assistant action audit (307 phrasings, `docs/ai-assistant-action-audit.md`, `tests/chat-action-audit.test.mjs`):** 85% first-time on the first set, 64% on unseen phrasing; rules widened, how-to questions no longer get cards, personal-data asks refused before contact requests; safety (no voting, money or admin buttons) held on every set. **Assistant Phase 3, tuning round 1 (no real chats yet):** intent gate audited with 46 phrasings (`tests/chat-intents.test.mjs`): 8 misses fixed (contact you, wrong-hours reports, taken off the site, show my lights); Tier 1 requests that read as off topic ("subscribe me", "this listing is outdated") now get their card without a model call; scope filter widened for 12 casual site questions (phone number, donations, QR code, photo size, code expired, two accounts, dog...); new KB chunk for photo formats and size. Real transcripts and Admin usage counts are still needed for the rest of Phase 3. **Assistant actions Phase 2 built** (signed-in cards for your own listings: take down or put back a home, mark an event cancelled or postponed, run it again next year, edit a description, withdraw a contest entry, add an event in one card; hand-offs to the home form and contest wizard; `functions/_lib/chatown.js`, `tests/chat-owner.test.mjs`; cards come from code and the widget makes one confirmed call to the site's own endpoints with the visitor's session; no voting, no money). Phase 2 does not need the Claude key yet because cards are chosen by code, not by the model. **Assistant actions Phase 1 built** (cards, Tier 1 and 2, Ask Human email, cap alerts and $10 cap via migration 0201, admin tier switches, Start this event hand-off, `tests/chat-actions.test.mjs`); legal wording drafted in `docs/ai-assistant-actions-legal-draft.md` and NOT yet in the Privacy and Terms pages; needs a Cloudflare preview check before merge. Design doc for assistant actions, Tiers 1 to 3, decisions made (direct Ask Human email with subject prefix `[SCL Ask Human]`, publish allowed, no voting, all tiers on, $10 cap with 80% and 100% alerts): `docs/ai-assistant-actions-design.md` (nothing built yet). Full knowledge audit (`docs/ai-assistant-audit.md`): 121 questions checked, 15 new chunks, `keywords:` support for chunks. Installers: six new chunks (all options, add a home, claim and manage, shared listings, quote requests, Sponsor steps). Store question: assistant says "No, not yet". Re-learned after the Tutorials/Help update (2026-11-10): contest wizard now Quick start vs Customize; adding an event needs sign-in (the Accounts chunk said it did not; privacy.html now says you sign in to add an event or a home (updated date November 10, 2026; attorney to review)); Favorites maps apps include Waze; weekly email distance choice; claim a home usually goes live at once. The `My night` rewrite was removed because sources are fixed. Knowledge base rebuilt. 20 target Q&As for review: `docs/ai-assistant-sample-qa.md` (includes a to-do list of missing KB chunks).

Draft-saving wording says Google or an emailed code; dashboard lists invite judges. **Assistant Phase 3, tuning round 1 (no real chats yet):** intent gate audited with 46 phrasings (`tests/chat-intents.test.mjs`): 8 misses fixed (contact you, wrong-hours reports, taken off the site, show my lights); Tier 1 requests that read as off topic ("subscribe me", "this listing is outdated") now get their card without a model call; scope filter widened for 12 casual site questions (phone number, donations, QR code, photo size, code expired, two accounts, dog...); new KB chunk for photo formats and size. Real transcripts and Admin usage counts are still needed for the rest of Phase 3. **Assistant actions Phase 2 built** (signed-in cards for your own listings: take down or put back a home, mark an event cancelled or postponed, run it again next year, edit a description, withdraw a contest entry, add an event in one card; hand-offs to the home form and contest wizard; `functions/_lib/chatown.js`, `tests/chat-owner.test.mjs`; cards come from code and the widget makes one confirmed call to the site's own endpoints with the visitor's session; no voting, no money). Phase 2 does not need the Claude key yet because cards are chosen by code, not by the model. **Assistant actions Phase 1 built** (cards, Tier 1 and 2, Ask Human email, cap alerts and $10 cap via migration 0201, admin tier switches, Start this event hand-off, `tests/chat-actions.test.mjs`); legal wording drafted in `docs/ai-assistant-actions-legal-draft.md` and NOT yet in the Privacy and Terms pages; needs a Cloudflare preview check before merge. Design doc for assistant actions, Tiers 1 to 3, decisions made (direct Ask Human email with subject prefix `[SCL Ask Human]`, publish allowed, no voting, all tiers on, $10 cap with 80% and 100% alerts): `docs/ai-assistant-actions-design.md` (nothing built yet). Full knowledge audit (`docs/ai-assistant-audit.md`): 121 questions checked, 15 new chunks, `keywords:` support for chunks. Installers: six new chunks (all options, add a home, claim and manage, shared listings, quote requests, Sponsor steps). Store question: assistant says "No, not yet". Re-learned after the Tutorials/Help update (2026-11-10): contest wizard now Quick start vs Customize; adding an event needs sign-in (the Accounts chunk said it did not; privacy.html now says you sign in to add an event or a home (updated date November 10, 2026; attorney to review)); Favorites maps apps include Waze; weekly email distance choice; claim a home usually goes live at once. The `My night` rewrite was removed because sources are fixed. Knowledge base rebuilt. 20 target Q&As for review: `docs/ai-assistant-sample-qa.md` (includes a to-do list of missing KB chunks).

## Outreach: sequence timing, backfill, deliverability pre-check (2026-11-08; owner decision)
Contract: `docs/monetization/grok-outreach-api.md` (updated), loop in the playbook 5b. Migration `0196_outreach_verify.sql` (`outreach_dns_cache`: domain, result, checked_at; `outreach_invalid`: address hash, reason). Additive and backward compatible.
- **`sequence` on every check result:** `{ next_step: 0|1|2|null, due, due_on, reason }` from the stored `sent` events (earliest per step, any segment). Original; follow-up 1 due original + 4 days; follow-up 2 due original + 10 days (Pacific dates, `time.js`); after step 2 `sequence_finished`, never restarts. Any reply/bounce/complaint/unsubscribe or a status other than clear ends it. `due` only when clear and due today or at most 14 days overdue (else `overdue_hold`). A follow-up with no original on file is `missing_original` (not sent, owner decides).
- **Backfill:** `POST /api/outreach/event` accepts `sent_at` (only with `sent`; real date, not future, within 120 days); one `sent` row per address and step, earliest date kept, repeats answer `duplicate:true`. New event `invalid` (+ `reason`) stores a hash only, does not suppress, and makes later checks answer the reason.
- **`verify: true`** adds `deliverability` ok | invalid_syntax | no_mail_server | disposable | unknown: strict syntax, about 50 built-in disposable domains, DNS-over-HTTPS (cloudflare-dns.com, domain only) MX then A then AAAA, 2 s timeout (`OUTREACH_DNS_TIMEOUT_MS` can only lower it, used by tests), concurrency 5, at most 38 DNS fetches per call, at most 20 distinct uncached domains (else 400 `hold:true`), cache in D1 for 24 hours (only definitive answers). Failure or timeout is `unknown`, never `no_mail_server`. Does NOT prove a mailbox exists (no SMTP probing); a paid verifier can be added if bounces pass about 2%.
- **Admin > Outreach reconcile:** shows how many are due today per step; the CSV has next step, due, due date and reason.
- **Privacy:** policy names Cloudflare's public DNS (domain name only), "Updated November 8, 2026"; retention deletes `outreach_invalid` after 24 months (now 9 retention statements). Tests: `tests/outreach-sequence.test.mjs`; the contract test in `outreach.test.mjs` now expects the `sequence` key.
- **Assumptions:** the 14-day overdue and day 4/10 rules are the owner's; a backfill earlier than a real send moves that step's date earlier; the DNS cache holds business domains only (free-mail domains are not looked up). **Risks:** DoH availability (falls back to `unknown`, sends continue); a very long 20-domain worst case may leave some domains `unknown` until the next call (the budget protects the 50-subrequest cap).

## Outreach API for Grok, suppression list, monthly update opt-in (2026-11-07; owner decision)
Contract and rules for Grok: `docs/monetization/grok-outreach-api.md` (hand it over as is); wording and the approved sequence (original, follow-up 1 about day 4, follow-up 2 about day 10, then stop) in `docs/monetization/grok-email-playbook.md` 5b; pilot plan in the playbook section 10. Migration `0195_outreach_newsletter.sql` (tables only, no data).
- **Least privilege:** Grok gets no database access and no personal data. `POST /api/outreach/check` (up to 50 addresses and optional domains) returns `{ i, email_hash_prefix, status, reasons }` per address: `clear | registered | listing | review | suppressed`; `POST /api/outreach/suppress`; `POST /api/outreach/event` (sent, reply, bounce, complaint, unsubscribe by step 0/1/2 and segment installer/contest/event_host; the last four also suppress). Auth `Authorization: Bearer <OUTREACH_API_KEY>` (Pages secret, constant-time compare, at least 16 characters); no secret = 503 `hold:true`, wrong or no key = 401, 60 calls a minute (429 `hold:true`), any lookup failure = 503 `hold:true`. Every call logs only counts to `ops_log` (kind `outreach`).
- **Hashes only:** SHA-256 of lowercased trimmed address + `|` + salt (`OUTREACH_SALT`, else `THROTTLE_SALT`, else a constant), in `outreach_suppressions` (plus an alias row for a `+tag`/Gmail-dots spelling) and `outreach_events`; no plaintext prospect address is stored. A check is one D1 batch per 50 addresses (about 3 outgoing calls with the rate limit and the log line), every statement at most 90 bound parameters.
- **What counts:** registered = `users.email`; listing = contact or owner email in event_submissions, posts, installers (owner_email, email), contests (contact_email, installer_email), event_claims, quote requests (contest_leads, installer_leads), contest_pass_leads, contest_staff, judges, contest_sponsors, alert_subs, feature_orders, home_consents, active newsletter_optins; suppressed = the list, `email_prefs.optout`, removal requests, newsletter unsubscribes; review = same domain as a registered installer/contest/listing website or user (free-mail domains never trigger it) or an invalid address.
- **App feeds the list:** weekly-alert unsubscribe (link and one-click), notification-email unsubscribe (`/api/unsub`), newsletter unsubscribe, and account deletion (`deleteAccount`, reason `manual`, source `account_deleted`) add the hash.
- **Admin > Outreach** (admin only): reconcile up to 500 addresses (counts by status and a CSV built in the browser, nothing stored), results by segment and step including registered/listed after being emailed (joined by hash at read time), suppression count and last 20 (hash prefix, reason, source, date), API health (key set, last check, 7-day counts).
- **Monthly update (separate opt-in):** `newsletter_optins` (role installer/host/family, topics, when, how, wording version, unsubscribed_at, token). Unchecked controls in Profile > Account and on the last step of add-home, add-event and add-contest; `/prefs?t=<token>` (no login) and one-click List-Unsubscribe via `/api/prefs`; `newsletterLinks()` gives a future sender the link and headers. The disclosure that sponsored or affiliate content will be identified is stored in `functions/_lib/newsletter.js` and shown with the controls. **The newsletter is not built or sent.** Deletion removes the row; retention deletes unsubscribed rows after 12 months and outreach events after 24 months.
- **Privacy:** `privacy.html` has "Monthly update (optional)" and "Contacting businesses and organizations, and opt-outs" and two retention lines; `tests/privacy.test.mjs` updated. Tests: `tests/outreach.test.mjs`.
- **Owner steps** (key, optional salt before first use, rotate, revoke): `docs/CHECKLIST.md` "Owner steps: outreach API". **Until `OUTREACH_API_KEY` exists the API answers 503 hold:true.**
- **Assumptions:** the salt must never change after first use; `review` is conservative (a shared company domain blocks the whole domain); `listing` also covers people who only asked for a quote or signed up for alerts; quote-request, judge and sponsor emails are matched only to avoid emailing people the app already knows (their data is never returned); empty topics mean "general update".
- **Risks:** a salt change loses suppression history; the stand-in D1 cannot prove real D1 behavior of a batch with about 25 to 50 statements (one outgoing call per slice); Admin conversion counts hash up to 1,500 recent rows per table per read (fine at pilot scale).

## Organizer-collected sponsors and sponsor invite links (2026-11-07; owner decision)
Details: `docs/contests.md` ("Sponsors collected by the organizer"), proposal and phases: `docs/monetization/contest-sponsor-payments-proposal.md` (phases 1 and 2 built; Stripe Connect Standard is still only a proposal). Migrations `0189_contest_sponsor_records.sql`, `0190_help_contest_sponsors.sql`.
- **The site is not a party to sponsor money.** The organizer records a paid (or in-kind) sponsor with contact, amount, "payment collected by me" (date, method) and a PRIVATE receipt (R2 `sponsor-receipt/...`, contest team and Admin only). A sponsor shows on the contest page ("Sponsor", logo, name, link) only when approved, payment confirmed (or in kind with a note), permission ticked and not hidden by Admin.
- **Safer path built:** organizer's own payment link/instructions (https only) and up to 3 display-only levels; a private invite link `/s/<token>` (on/off/new); the public sponsor form (no account, Turnstile, honeypot, rate limits, noindex, SVG refused); a review queue (Mark payment received, Approve, Decline with email, Remove); three editable emails. Only for live contests whose organizer carries no review note; Admin can switch the link off and hide payment text or a sponsor.
- **Privacy:** privacy.html and Terms updated; account deletion and a 24-month retention purge remove receipts and contact details.
- **Assumptions:** "trusted" means the contest has no review note (matched website or Admin "Looks fine"), which is stricter than "not free/none" because a mismatched website also carries a note; a receipt is optional; the amount is the organizer's own record; one payment link per contest; a sponsor row never carries over payment or permission when a contest is run again.
- **Tests:** `tests/contest-sponsors.test.mjs`, `tests/sponsor-invite.test.mjs`; browser check of the organizer tab, the sponsor page, the contest page and the Admin view at 390 and 1280 px, light and dark.
- **Not built:** Stripe Connect, Plaid, any money movement.

## Contest passes (2026-11-07; owner decision, billing starts now)
Rules, files and Admin steps: `docs/contests.md` ("Contest passes"). Migrations `0187_contest_passes.sql` (schema, no data) and `0188_help_contest_passes.sql` (Help, seed-safe).
- **Rule:** each organization gets 1 free contest per 12-month window; Up to 5 ($50), Up to 10 ($75), Up to 20 ($100) are TOTAL contests in the window (free one included); more than 20 is "contact us" (mailto plus an Admin-visible lead). Upgrade = price difference. Prices only in `functions/_lib/passes.js`, served by `/api/config` as `contestPasses`.
- **Organization key:** contest website's registrable domain when it matches the organizer's verified email domain (`d:covinaca.gov`), else the organizer's account email (`u:`). Counting uses `contests.live_at` (set the first time a contest is live); live-then-unpublished still counts; drafts, held and declined-before-live do not.
- **Paywall:** `POST /api/contests` and "Run it again" answer 402 with the picker data; the wizard shows the picker (Choose buttons, full contents, window dates, count used), keeps the form in the browser, pays on Stripe and creates the contest by itself on return. New page `/contest-pass` for buying ahead.
- **Payment:** same Checkout/webhook/orders (`target='contest_pass'`), quote locked, replay safe, amount checked, stale or duplicate pass = refund_due, refund/dispute before the extra contests were used revokes the pass, after use it flags the order for Admin. Email template `contest_pass_paid`. Counters `fn:pass-picker-shown`, `fn:pass-choose`, `fn:pass-paid`.
- **Admin:** Contests > Contest passes (usage, comps, passes, leads, merge, revoke); Orders shows pass rows. No comp is seeded: Covina is granted by hand (steps in `docs/contests.md`).
- **Assumptions:** Admin approval of a held contest always goes through (flagged "over its limit"); Admin deleting a live contest lowers the count; a comp's number is total contests per window; a refund after use is decided by Admin; a Gmail owner is an individual even with a city website.
- **Tests:** `tests/contest-passes.test.mjs`; browser check at 390 and 1280 px, light and dark (pricing, wizard paywall, return after payment with the signed webhook posted by a script, pass page, Admin panel, Orders): axe clean except the pre-existing `#gsq` search box on Admin at 1280. Videos out of date: `docs/tutorial-updates.md` (quick, custom).
- **Next:** PR B (organizer-collected sponsors) and `docs/monetization/contest-sponsor-payments-proposal.md`; attorney review of `legal-redlines.md` "Contest organizer passes".

## New pricing structure (2026-11-06; owner decision, migrations 0183 and 0184)
- **Events:** free listing; Featured pin, Banner + pin, Top of the list + pin, all three. Additive $25 components (`COMPONENT_CENTS`, `EVENT_PLANS` in `functions/_lib/featured.js`; keys `ev_pin`, `ev_banner`, `ev_top`, `ev_all`). Window: from payment to the event's last day or the season end (Christmas lights Jan 6), whichever comes first; opens 30 days before the event. Quote (dates, price) locked at checkout (`eventQuote`) and stored on the order; webhook checks the amount, holds a duplicate as refund due. Upgrades charge only for components added; nothing new = 400. Old keys `pin`/`top`/`season` are no longer sold for events but still apply, refund and restore.
- **Event banner:** `events.banner_until` (0183), `feature_orders.applied_banner_until`. `/api/banner` returns event pills (`ev: 1`); `public/banner.js` shows up to 3 per view, matched to events whose pin is in the map view, labelled "Featured", tap opens the event. Shown/taps in `banner_stats` under `ev:<id>`, shown to the organizer on /feature (`/api/me/featurable` `banner`). Refund, dispute, partial refund, restore and the refund-due helpers cover the banner date. Admin: events editor has "Paid placement" (level, Featured until, Banner until) with a floor from running paid orders (`GET /api/admin/events/<id>`, `placement` on save); Admin > Orders shows plan, parts and dates.
- **Installers:** new plan `top` ($50, card WITHOUT the banner, `top_until` only). Upgrade to both from either single product is the difference ($25); the other single product is a full separate purchase; /sponsor says "Both together are ... upgrade for ...". `/api/banner` marks strip members `bn: 1`; auto-renewal renews the plan bought (top-only renews as top-only).
- **Pages:** `/pricing`, `/feature` (plan picker and confirm built from `public/feature-plans.js`, quotes from the server), `/add-event` plan step (`GET /api/feature/quote`), `/sponsor`, success view, `feature_paid` email (`list` variable), Terms "Paid placements", Feature FAQ, Help articles (0184, seed-only, no prices in them). Strip's dashed pill now "Advertise here" -> `/pricing`. Homes unchanged ($10 pin, $15 top, 30 days, podium only).
- **Tests:** `tests/event-tiers.test.mjs`, `tests/installer-top.test.mjs`, a renewal test, and rewritten old-plan tests (`checkout`, `featured-window`, `frontend-logic`, `event-funnel`, `terms`). Browser check at 390 and 1280 px, light and dark: pricing, feature, sponsor, add-event, map with an event pill; axe 0 violations on all.
- **Assumptions and risks:** see the PR description. Prices are not written in Help articles. A saved (edited) `feature_paid` email template keeps its old wording until reset in Admin > Settings > Email templates. Videos out of date: `docs/tutorial-updates.md`.
- **Next:** owner re-records videos; attorney review of `docs/monetization/legal-redlines.md` "Pricing and tier wording changed 2026-11-06".

## Audit fixes: private homes, fake contests, installer takeover, email caps, admin CSRF (2026-11-07; PL-01, N-02, S-04, N-03, S-10, S-08)
Migration `0181_audit_privacy_abuse.sql` (add-only). Tests: `tests/audit-fixes.test.mjs`, updated `tests/privacy.test.mjs`.
- **Decision reversed (owner priority after the audit).** The 2026-11-06 note that a "remove this listing" request "was left out on purpose" (see "Is this your home? Claim it" below) no longer holds: residents now have a Remove my home path.
- **PL-01 consent record.** Every confirmed "add homes" call writes one `home_consents` row (organizer user id and email, contest, number of homes, `CONSENT_VERSION`, the exact `CONSENT_TEXT`, time) and links each home through `posts.consent_id`. `entry_add_log` is cleaned daily so it cannot hold this. The organizer checkbox now shows the same sentence (a test keeps them identical; change the wording only with a new version).
- **PL-01 Remove my home.** Link "Not your listing? Remove my home" on the map detail of every home and of "Private home (view from the street)" listings, and on `/e/<id>` for those listings, opens `/remove-home` (listing id prefilled, or street address, email, optional note, Turnstile). `POST /api/remove-home`: same-origin, 5 an hour per connection (`submit_log`, pruned in 48 hours), 3 a day per address (hashed), honeypot. It stores a `removal_requests` row, emails the team (`home_removal_admin`) and the requester (`home_removal_ack`). Admin > Reports now has a "Remove my home requests" block: Approve deletes the listing (an event row, or a home with its photos, votes, ballots and frozen-result entry), marks it done and emails the requester (`home_removal_done`); address-only requests show address matches to pick from, or "Nothing to remove, close it"; Decline emails the reason (`home_removal_declined`). Open requests count in the Reports tab, the Dashboard and the daily digest. A paid Featured placement that was running is flagged refund due.
- **PL-01 privacy policy.** New section "Homes listed by our team or by contest organizers" plus two retention bullets. Updated date unchanged (November 6, 2026).
- **N-02.** An organizer we cannot tie to a website (free-mail, no site, mismatch) whose contest name or organizer uses an official-sounding word (city, town of, county, municipal, HOA, homeowners, chamber, school, unified, PTA, official, sheriff, police) now starts `pending` (not on the map, cannot add homes or buy placements) with a note, and the admin gets a `contest_review` email at once; approve in Admin > Contests (the organizer then gets the contest-is-on-the-map email). Any other contest still goes live instantly. A live contest with a review note is capped at 25 organizer-added homes until "Looks fine" (`UNTRUSTED_ADDED_CAP`). The organizer sees "We will review your contest, usually within a day".
- **S-04.** `functions/_lib/installers.js`: an existing profile is matched by name, slug or website host (shared hosts such as facebook.com never match) and is never duplicated or changed by a post unless the poster owns it (`owner_email`) or created it (`created_by`). Other installer posts attach as unverified (`installer_confirmed = 0` plus a token) and the profile contact gets the confirm email. `installers.web_src = 'user'` marks a website typed by a visitor: email-domain auto-claim works only for an Admin-set website or for the creator; otherwise the claim goes to Admin. Admin editing the website makes it Admin-set. Installer pages no longer show "Confirmed" just because the poster typed "installer".
- **N-03 / S-10.** `functions/_lib/throttle.js` + table `send_throttle` (hashed keys, rows deleted after 48 hours on every insert): quote emails (installer and contest) 5 a day and 1 a minute per account, 10 a day per connection; weekly-alert confirmation emails 3 a day per address, 20 a day per connection (only when an email would be sent).
- **S-08.** `functions/api/admin/_middleware.js` refuses POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE whose Origin (or, with no Origin, Referer) is another site; requests with neither header and all reads work as before. `public/admin/admin.js` calls are same-origin already; workflows use key-protected non-admin routes.
- **Not done / open:** consent evidence for homes the team lists itself; a suppression list so a later city sweep does not re-add a removed home (sweep authors: check Admin > Reports > handled requests, `docs/updating-events.md`); the `already` field of `/api/alerts` still shows whether an address is subscribed; profiles seeded by migration 0140 keep `web_src = ''` (trusted as before).

_Last updated: 2026-11-06 (refund flags, checkout holds, retention)_

## Refund flags, checkout holds, retention (2026-11-06, audit engineering H1, payments M3/M4/M8, privacy PL-04)
- **One helper** in `functions/_lib/orders.js`: `flagRefundDue` / `refundDueStatements` (paid or held, unrefunded, unapplied-or-running orders become `refund_due` with an `[auto]` note, so they show in Admin > Orders, the tab badge and the digest email), `restoreRefundDue`, `takeOffMap` / `putBackOnMap`. Ids are sliced under the 100-parameter limit; statements go in the caller's own batch (before the delete).
- **Paths now flagged:** account deletion (the contest owner's event, their homes, their installer profile's Sponsor orders), `DELETE /api/me/homes/<id>`, `DELETE /api/me/events/<id>`, a contest creator's Unpublish, Admin contest decline and delete (also the contest's podium homes), Admin installer Hide (M4; no confirm step, flagged at once). Unpublish, decline and Hide are restorable: Publish again, Approve and Show turn those orders back to `paid` and `restorePlacement` puts the placement back (an order the owner already marked refunded stays refunded). Deletes are final. The existing Admin event, home and submission paths were already flagged and are unchanged.
- **Guard test:** `tests/refund-flags.test.mjs` scans `functions/` and fails when a file deletes an event, home or installer row (or hides an installer) without using the helper; plus a test per path.
- **Checkout (M3, M8):** `POST /api/feature/checkout` returns 400 for a cancelled or postponed event (direct event id and a published submission). **Decision for `expected` events (projected dates):** refused with a clear message ("dates are still projected, not confirmed ... email info@socallights.app") when the buyer holds only a claim, because a claim gives no edit rights and the paid window is computed from dates they cannot correct. Not held: an event the buyer submitted themselves (their own dates, editable) and a contest's map listing. Admin unblocks a claimed listing by confirming its dates. Sponsor checkout has no event (installers have no `notice`), and homes have none either, so nothing to check there. The Feature page shows the 400 text; it has no special state for it yet.
- **Retention (PL-04):** account deletion blanks `admin_log.label` (and the Users delete entry an admin's own delete writes) and the person's address in co-organizer lines of `contest_changes` (all co-organizer lines of contests they ran). `functions/_lib/retention.js` `pruneRetention` (run by the admin digest, 3 times a day): unconfirmed `alert_subs` deleted after 30 days; declined `event_submissions.contact_email`, `reports` name/message/contact, `admin_log` labels of Users/Reports areas and co-organizer `contest_changes` old/new blanked after 12 months. `public/privacy.html` is unchanged (nothing it promises is false; the new windows are not stated there yet, for the attorney and the privacy-page agent).
- **PL-01 tables (PR 405):** `home_consents` is evidence that residents agreed to be listed, so on account deletion the rows stay (contest, count, wording, date) with `user_id` and `email` blanked; deleting the contest deletes its consent rows. `removal_requests`: an account deletion blanks email and note of that address's closed requests; the purge blanks address, email and note 6 months after Admin closed a request (open ones are kept). `installers.created_by` is blanked on deletion. `send_throttle` holds hashes only and is already pruned at 48 hours. `public/privacy.html` not changed.

Every workflow reports at the end (`if: always()`, never breaks the job). Stale or failed jobs: Admin home red banner (`admin.js?v=20261107m`), digest section "Scheduled jobs and deploys" (subject `STALE JOBS`), `POST /api/usage-check` `stale` list, GitHub issue "Scheduled job stopped reporting" (`.github/scripts/stale-issue.sh`, run by usage-check and digest), and a late backup (30 h) is started by that script.

## Free-plan per-request limits (2026-11-06, audit M9, M2, C13)
Verified limits and the full before/after table: `docs/runbooks/cloudflare-limits.md` ("Per-request limits and how the code stays under them"). Key fact: on Workers/Pages Free a request may make **50 subrequests and D1 and R2 calls count too** (a D1 `batch()` is one); D1 allows 100 bound parameters per query.
- **Mail outbox.** Migration `0179_mail_outbox.sql`, `functions/_lib/mailbatch.js` (batched Resend `/emails/batch`, outbox with dedupe, retry/backoff, split of a refused batch, quota pause), `POST /api/mail/drain`, hourly workflow `.github/workflows/mail-outbox.yml`. Contest results, voting reminders: queued, first 40 sent at once (CPU: 10 ms on Free), rest by the hourly workflow in a loop. Invitations, judge mail, team notices and the weekly alerts (40 per run) use one batch call (`sendNow`). Failures are logged to `ops_log` (`email`, `mail`), never swallowed.
- **Organizer-added entries:** 10 homes per request (was 100, UI 25), UI chunks of 10 with progress, hourly limit by homes (1,000) and calls (120); test asserts under 50 outgoing calls with both geocoders hit.
- **`/api/posts`:** winners read through `selectIn` (slices of 90) and `winnerBadges` in one pass; failures logged (`posts`, `badges`); `public/app.js` boot uses `Promise.allSettled` (index.html `?v=20261107l`).
- **R2 deletes** (account, contest, home) in one call per 1,000 keys; change logs in one batch; D1 stand-in (`tests/_d1.mjs`) now enforces 100 parameters and 100 KB statements and counts calls (`DB.stats`).
- **Tests:** `tests/limits.test.mjs`, `tests/limits-r2.test.mjs`, updated `tests/organizer-entries.test.mjs`.
- **Open:** sponsor renewals job still does about 6 to 10 calls per renewal (about 5 per run on Free; rows stay due, see the runbook); Resend free daily quota is 100 emails; confirm the Cloudflare plan; not tested with real email.

## /api/events hardening (2026-11-06, audit N-01 / M1)
- The edge copy is keyed by `ended` and `full` only; any other query string (`?x=1`, `?ref=fb`) shares the plain copy and cannot force a rebuild. At most 4 keys exist.
- `?all=1` is admin only (Access JWT via `requireAdmin`, else 401, never cached). The admin editor now reads `GET /api/admin/events` (behind Access, same data); `admin.js?v=20261107d`.
- A stale copy gets a 20 second lease (the first request re-stores it with a younger age) plus a same-isolate in-flight flag, so concurrent requests start one rebuild. Best effort across edge locations.
- Tests: `tests/events-api.test.mjs`. Still open: one Cloudflare WAF rate-limit rule on `/api/*` (dashboard, owner).

## Sign-in code attempt limit (2026-11-06, audit S-07)
- `functions/api/auth/email/verify.js`: an attempt is spent with one `UPDATE login_codes SET tries = tries + 1 WHERE id = ? AND tries < 5` and `meta.changes` is checked before the code is compared, so parallel guesses cannot exceed 5 per code. A per-address cap of 30 verifies an hour (429) counts rows in `submit_log` keyed `vf:<24 hex of a SHA-256 of the address>`; no raw address is stored, and the existing 48 hour prune removes them. No migration.
- Tests: `tests/signin-attempts.test.mjs` (the stand-in database is synchronous, so it reproduces the old race by interleaving awaits but not real multi-connection D1 contention).
- Not done: deleting older codes when a new one is issued (not needed: verify only reads the newest code).

A hit reads 0 rows. Output is byte-identical to the old code (tested against the old implementation); any error serves the plain static page.

## Cloudflare free-plan usage panel and alerts (2026-11-06)
Runbook with exact owner steps: `docs/runbooks/cloudflare-limits.md`.
- **Panel.** Admin > Cloudflare usage (`GET /api/admin/usage`, admin only, cached 5 min in D1 table `cf_usage_cache`, migration 0178): today's UTC usage vs the free limit for D1 rows read (5M/day) and written (100k/day), Functions requests (100k/day), R2 Class A (1M/month) and Class B (10M/month), D1 and R2 storage, Resend emails (100/day, 3,000/month, counted by our own counter `fn:email-sent` in `sendMail`), Function CPU errors; 7-day trend per metric; colors at 70 / 90 / 100%. Limits and source URLs are in one module, `functions/_lib/cflimits.js`; all numbers verified 2026-11-06.
- **Data.** Cloudflare GraphQL Analytics, read-only, needs Pages secrets `CF_ANALYTICS_TOKEN` (Account Analytics: Read) and `CF_ACCOUNT_ID`. Without them the panel shows "Not connected" with the setup steps. **Owner has not set them yet.**
- **Alerts.** Digest email section (top metric always, 70%+ listed, subject `LIMIT` at 90%+); `POST /api/usage-check` (key `ALERT_CRON_KEY`, 401 otherwise) + `.github/workflows/usage-check.yml` every 30 min (and the digest workflow runs it too) opens/updates/closes the issue "Cloudflare free-plan limit reached or near" via `.github/scripts/usage-issue.sh`.
- **Runtime detection.** `logOp` files quota-like messages (D1 exceeded / rows read / daily limit, Too many subrequests, Error 1027, Resend daily_quota_exceeded) as kind `limit` (deduped 10 min); `_middleware.js` notes uncaught ones; Admin home shows a banner for 24 h.
- **Finding.** On 2026-11-06 at ~11:20 UTC D1 rows read was already 3.66M of 5M (73%); the last 6 days were 1.3M to 3.7M a day. Function CPU p50 is above the free 10 ms with no CPU errors, so the account may be on Workers Paid: check Billing.
- **Tests.** `tests/cf-usage.test.mjs` (stubbed GraphQL). Browser check (local wrangler, stub server, 390 and 1280, light and dark): panel axe clean; the only axe item on Admin is the pre-existing `aria-allowed-attr` on the search box at 1280. `admin.js` and `admin.css` `?v=20261107e`.

## Optional automatic renewal for installer Sponsor placements (2026-11-07)
Installers only, default OFF. Details: `docs/installer-banner.md` (Optional automatic renewal), Stripe steps in `docs/stripe-setup.md`, attorney notes in `docs/monetization/legal-redlines.md`.
- **Flow:** unticked box on `/sponsor` (or a dashboard toggle that opens a Stripe card-setup page) -> consent wording (versioned) stored -> Stripe keeps the card, we keep only ids (`installer_autorenew`, migration 0176) -> reminder email 7 days before -> on the last day of the running placement a daily job (`.github/workflows/sponsor-renewals.yml`, existing `ALERT_CRON_KEY`) creates one off-session PaymentIntent at the plan's current price -> the order is applied through the same machinery as a Checkout payment (keyed by the PaymentIntent id; `payment_intent.succeeded` webhook is the safety net) -> next season starts the next day, one season ahead at most. Failure: email, one retry after 3 days, then lapse. Cancel by the link in any email (`/cancel-renewal`, no login) or in the dashboard. Refund or dispute pauses it.
- **Also:** admin Orders "Automatic renewals" list and "Auto-renewal" tag, digest line, counters `fn:autorenew-*`, account deletion detaches the card, six editable emails plus a team notice, Terms/Privacy/Pricing/Help updated.
- **Audit fixes shipped with it:** H2 (Pacific date for banners, real DST), M1 (quote locked at checkout; stale second sessions become refund_due, never double-booked), M2 ($25 difference when the banner already covers the period), PL-02 (copy), ARL checklist; deferred items are listed in `docs/installer-banner.md`.
- **Tests:** `tests/sponsor-renewal.test.mjs`, `tests/sponsor-audit.test.mjs`. **Open:** a real $50 renewal test (owner steps in the report and `docs/stripe-setup.md`); attorney review of the ARL notes; add `payment_intent.succeeded` to the Stripe webhook.

## Installer Sponsor placements: pay for the banner and top of the list (2026-11-06)
Owner-approved: installers buy the **Banner ($50 per season)** and **Banner and top of the list ($75 per season)** themselves. Details and rules: `docs/installer-banner.md` (Payments).
- **Funnel:** `/pricing#banner` Choose buttons (aligned, named like "Choose Banner, $50") to `/sponsor` (sign in, profile, plan, exact dates and price, Stripe), return states `?paid=1` and `?cancelled=1`; dashboard card with Renew or upgrade.
- **Season and price rule (final, no proration):** a plan costs its full price (Banner $50, Banner and top $75) whenever it is bought during a season, and runs until the season's last day (sponsor seasons: Christmas lights Nov 3 to Jan 6, New Year's Jan 7 to Jan 31, the rest as the map windows; the map's `public/seasons.js` is unchanged). The page shows the exact dates, the number of days and the price before payment. Renewal starts the day after a running placement ends (full price), one season ahead at most. Upgrade: the difference between the two plans' prices ($25) while the banner runs to the season end; no credit or refund for timing. The earlier proration, option C and per-plan floors were removed (`installerPrice`, `min_cents`, `installerUpgradeMinCents` are gone). Terms updated to match. Code: `functions/_lib/sponsor.js`, prices in `INSTALLER_PLANS`.
- **Server:** migration 0171, `/api/sponsor/checkout`, `/api/me/sponsor`, webhook orders `target='installer'` (apply, replay, refund, partial, dispute, dispute won, refund first), amount checked against the same calculation, admin Orders row, admin date floor, merge keeps orders, emails `sponsor_paid` and `sponsor_admin`, funnel counters `fn:pricing-choose`, `fn:sponsor-shown`, `fn:sponsor-picked`, `fn:sponsor-checkout`, `fn:sponsor-paid`.

## Lighter event list: blurb and link load on open (2026-11-06)
Owner-approved. `GET /api/events` no longer sends each event's blurb (`b`) and link (`u`); the map loads them from the new `GET /api/events/<id>` when an event's details open.
- **Numbers (1,254 events, same data, local run):** raw 863,988 to 619,450 bytes (-28%); gzip -6 210,150 to 137,381 (-35%). Per-field cost measured on the live-shaped data (raw / gzip): `b` 153 KB / 47 KB, `img` 98 KB / 30 KB, `u` 93 KB / 21 KB, `a` 59 KB / 18 KB, `h` 46 KB / 11 KB, `imgs` 13.5 KB / 0.5 KB.
- **Why only `b` and `u`.** The app code decides it: `img` draws the card photos; `a` is searched (search matches name, city, county and address, never the blurb) and is the route and directions destination, and goes in the calendar location; `h` shows on every card and sets calendar times; `v`, `p`, `lc`, `nt` show on cards or tags. `b` is read only by the details page, plus the Contests type filter (which also matches a contest or competition named in the blurb): the list now carries `ct: 1` for those events (185 of 1,254, identical to the old test). `u` is read only by the details page, the calendar note and the photo credit.
- **Server.** `rowToListEvent` in `functions/_lib/util.js` (list), `functions/api/events/[id].js` (full object, ETag and 304, `max-age=60, s-maxage=120`, edge copy; 404 JSON for an unknown id; ended events still open). `?full=1`, `?ended=1` and `?all=1` still return full objects (admin editor, scripts). The edge copy, stale-while-revalidate and ETag of the list are unchanged.
- **App (`app.js?v=20261107g`, `styles.css?v=20261107g`).** Opening an event (list, pin card, `#e-<id>`, `/e/<id>` button) fetches the details once and keeps them in memory (kept across the 15-minute list refresh). The page renders at once; the blurb shows a grey block as tall as a three-line blurb, the Event info button has its space reserved, then both are filled in place (no redraw, photo and scroll stay). Calendar waits for the details so the event link is in the note. Failed fetch or no signal: "Details need a connection." (or "Couldn't load the details." for a server error) with Try again; title, dates, hours, price and Directions still show.
- **Service worker (`scl-5`).** Details of events you opened are saved in a second cache `scld-5` (120 newest), network first like the lists, so an opened event still opens offline. The offline page reads the newest `scl-*` cache, which is why the details cache is not named `scl-...`.
- **Other consumers.** Admin (`?all=1`), `/e/<id>`, `/past`, sitemap, city pages read the database, not this endpoint. `feature.html`, `add-house.html` (claim) and `find-plan.mjs` only use fields still in the list. `scripts/photo_pass.py` needs `u` and now calls `?full=1`.
- **Tests.** `tests/event-detail.test.mjs` (list omits `b`/`u`, `ct`, full on request, detail has all fields and equals the full entry, 404, ETag/304, edge copy). Real-browser run (Chrome via Playwright, `wrangler pages dev` with a local D1 of all migrations, phone 390 and desktop 1280): list open, `#e-` deep link, `/e/<id>` button, slow and failed fetch with Try again, address search, Contests filter count, My night route addresses, Google and .ics calendar, offline after opening an event (and the note for one never opened).
- **Risk.** A tab left open across the deploy runs the old `app.js` against the new list and would show empty blurbs until it is reloaded (the `?v=` bump covers every new page load). Tapping calendar before the details arrive, with Google Calendar chosen, waits for the fetch, so a phone browser could block the pop-up in that rare case.

- Automated sweep of 22 pages x phone/desktop x light/dark plus a seeded contest, and a crawl of 409 links (0 broken). Nine gaps fixed; open items and what needs a real device or an owner decision are in `docs/audit-2026-11-06.md`.
## Featured event pricing (2026-11-06)
Owner-approved: the window follows the event. Featured pin $10 and Top of the list $15 run from payment to the event's last day (`end_date` as stored when paid, fixed then), capped at 30 days: `min(end_date, today + 30)`. Checkout refuses an event that starts more than 30 days away (400, "Featuring opens 30 days before your event: come back on <date>"); a running event is fine. Whole season $20 is replaced by **Full run $25** (level 2; events of 31+ days that still have 7+ days left; to the last day, capped at 90 days). The stored plan key stays `season` so old orders and the refund code keep working; `PLANS.season.label` is "Full run"; paid $20 orders are grandfathered (no cap, no amount-mismatch flag). Homes unchanged ($10 / $15, 30 days, Full run refused). A repeat purchase that adds nothing (same or lower level, already through the cap or last day) is refused with a 400; an upgrade of level is still sold; the stacking rule never shortens and never lowers the level.
- **Code.** `functions/_lib/featured.js` (`PLANS`, `applyPlan`, `eventPlanProblem`, `OPEN_DAYS`), `functions/api/feature/checkout.js`, `functions/api/stripe-webhook.js`, `/api/config` (adds `featureOpenDays`, `minLength`, `maxDays`). `recomputeAfterRevoke` and `restorePlacement` work from stored `applied_until`, so they needed no change; tests cover them under the cap (`tests/featured-window.test.mjs`).
- **Copy.** New shared module `public/feature-plans.js` (`?v=20261106z` on `/feature`, `/pricing`, add-event): every tier lists its contents in full, never "Everything in ...". Event wording is "event", home wording is "home".
- **Help.** Migration `0168_help_featured_pricing.sql` (from `scripts/help/updates-0168.mjs`) updates `is-it-free` and `feature-an-event-or-home` only where `updated_by = 'seed'`; `docs/help-updates.md` has the paste-ready text for edited articles.
- **Docs.** `finance-pricing.md` (section 2b), `legal-redlines.md` (note for the attorney), `stripe-setup.md`, `monetization.md`, `finance-operations.md`, `CHECKLIST.md` (owner to-dos).
- **Risk.** No real payment was made. Terms still do not mention paid placements (attorney item).

- Automated sweep of 22 pages x phone/desktop x light/dark plus a seeded contest, and a crawl of 409 links (0 broken). Nine gaps fixed; open items and what needs a real device or an owner decision are in `docs/audit-2026-11-06.md`.

"Skip to the content" link (`footer.js`) on every non-map page with a `<main>`; map top bar is a `<header>` before the canvas (Tab reaches search first); `role="banner"` on page headers; `<main>` on Profile; `aria-invalid` plus `aria-describedby` on the first empty field of an add-flow step that shows an error and on the co-organizer email; footer links 44 px tall. ## Batch E 4: accessibility quick fixes (2026-11-06)
- Search box `#q` is now a proper combobox (`role="combobox"`, `aria-controls="sugg"`, `aria-autocomplete="list"`, `aria-haspopup="listbox"`); suggestions have ids and `aria-activedescendant` follows the arrow keys. This removes the invalid `aria-expanded` on `<input type="search">` (audit F4).
- Heading order on the map: a hidden `<h2>Places in view</h2>` (class `sr`) above the cards, and the filter sheet's section titles are `h3` instead of `h4` (same CSS rule, `.fbody h3,.fbody h4`). No visual change.
- `<main>` on the Add hub, the three sign-in gates and every step of the add flow (`addflow.js`, `add*.html`); `.main` class styling unchanged (only `addflow.css` loads on those pages, which has no `main` tag rule).
- `app.js?v=20261106v`, `styles.css?v=20261106t`. `addflow.js` has no version bump (4-hour cache; the change is invisible).
- Not done, and why: contrast of the coral "Live" tab on `/contests` and the light-theme "Confirmed" tag (needs a colour change people would see; propose #d93f3f for the owner to approve), skip link on non-map pages, `<header>` landmark on the map, aria-invalid on form errors, tap-target padding on footer links. Not checked with a screen reader or axe after the change (no browser in this session).

## Batch E 3: per-event pages with share previews (2026-11-06)
- `GET /e/<event id>` (`functions/e/[id].js`, `/e/*` added to `public/_routes.json`): a plain server page per event with its own title, description, canonical, `og:url`, `og:type`, `twitter:card` and `og:image` (the event's photo, else `share.png`), the facts, "Open on the map" (`/#e-<id>`), and links to the city page, `/in` and Add. It is a new page type: the map and its look are unchanged.
- schema.org Event data only for confirmed events that have not ended (price offer when free or a `$` price is parsed; cancelled/postponed set from the event notice). Expected and ended events are `noindex,follow` and carry no Event data. Unknown id: 404.
- The map's Share button on an event now shares `/e/<id>` (houses still share `/#h-<id>`), so Messages, Slack and social posts show the event's own card. `app.js?v=20261106u`.
- `page()` in `functions/_lib/shell.js` now writes `og:url` (from canonical), `og:type`, `og:site_name`, `twitter:card` and takes an `image`: every city, help, installer and past page gains these (audit F9).
- Sitemap lists confirmed upcoming events; the hidden homepage list adds 20 confirmed events linking to `/e/<id>`. City pages still link events to the map.
- Tests: `tests/event-page.test.mjs`. Not done: house share pages, an auto-redirect to the map (would make Google treat the page as a redirect), BreadcrumbList.

## Batch E 2: funnel counters (2026-11-06)
- New `functions/_lib/funnel.js`: steps stored in `site_hits` under keys `fn:*` (a number per day; no IP, cookie, account or text). Counted on the server, at the real completion: `fn:signin-done` (`startSession`), `fn:add-event-done`, `fn:add-house-done`, `fn:contest-entry-done`, `fn:subscribe` (new sign-ups only), `fn:subscribe-confirm`. Counted in the browser (allowlist in `/api/visit`, skipped when `navigator.webdriver`): `fn:event-open` (event card opened, `app.js` `openDetail`), `fn:signin-open` (`auth.js`), `fn:visit` (first page of a tab session, `hit.js`).
- `/api/visit` page names: once a day has 80 different names, new ones are ignored (anyone could create unlimited rows before); page keys now have the same daily cap as click keys.
- "Real humans": there was no such number in the app, only in the strategy docs. The dashboard's "Page views" is now labelled "Page views*" with a note saying it is a per-tab-session count from a script, not people. `fn:visit` gives a second, coarser number to compare for a week. Dashboard page-view totals and "most visited" no longer include `fn:*` and `ref:*` keys.
- Admin home has a "How people use the site" list (today and 7 days); the admin digest email has a "How people used the site" section. Tests: `tests/funnel.test.mjs`.
- `app.js?v=` bumped to 20261106t. `auth.js` was changed but its `?v=` (14 files) was not bumped: browsers get it within 4 hours, and until then only `fn:signin-open` is missed.
- Still open: Cloudflare analytics comparison (needs the token), `click:directions`, `add:*-start` counters, install counters.

## Batch E 1: city pages (2026-11-06)
- `functions/_lib/cityindex.js` is the one place for city logic. A city page is indexable only with 3+ upcoming events or at least one confirmed one; the rest are `noindex,follow` (shell `page()` now writes `noindex,follow`) and are left out of `/sitemap.xml`. They still open normally from the map and links.
- City pages show the 6 nearest indexable cities with distances, the rest of the county, a one-line count and date-span summary, and links to `/in`, Add an event, Pricing and Help.
- New `/in` hub (`functions/in/index.js`): all cities by county, in the sitemap. The homepage is now served through `functions/index.js`, which adds a visually hidden (`.sr`, links `tabindex=-1`) "Browse by city" list at the end of `<body>`; the visible map is unchanged and `app.js`/`styles.css` untouched. On any error the plain page is served. `/` and `/in` added to `public/_routes.json`.
- Tests: `tests/cities.test.mjs`. Not done: county hub pages, a crawlable events list on the homepage (comes with the per-event pages), `Allow: /api/events` in robots.
## Claim a home (2026-11-06)
- "Is this your home? Claim it" on sweep-made home listings opens Add a display prefilled with that listing; the admin sees "Says this is their home" on the display. Replacement still follows the same-address rule. Migration 0164. See `docs/superseding.md`. A "remove this listing" request was left out on purpose.

## Batch D 1: nightly backup (2026-11-06)
`.github/workflows/backup.yml` exports the live D1 every night, checks it, uploads it to a PRIVATE R2 bucket `socal-lights-backups` (daily kept 35 days, monthly 400, `latest.sql.gz`), reads it back, and copies photos on Sundays (`scripts/backup-photos.mjs`). A failure opens a GitHub issue. Never an artifact (public repo). Restore steps: `docs/runbooks/backup-restore.md`. Owner must give the API token R2 Edit permission first.

## Batch D 4: offline beyond the map (2026-11-06)
- The service worker (`public/sw.js`, cache `scl-4`) is now registered from every page: `public/footer.js` (all pages but the map, which registers it in `index.html`; `footer.js?v=` bumped to `20261106a` in 19 places). On install it saves, when reachable, `/`, `/contests`, `/pricing`, `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/tutorials` and the two event/house lists; any page visited later is saved too (newest 80 kept; redirects and errors never saved).
- Offline, a saved page opens; an unsaved one shows `public/offline.html`, which now lists the pages still available on the device, says when the event list was saved, retries by itself when the connection returns, and has "Try again". If a saved copy exists and the network has not answered in 6 seconds (weak signal), the saved copy is used. The map library from unpkg is cached so the map page does not break without it (map tiles still need a connection).
- Root cause of the audit's blank error offline: Cloudflare Pages redirects `/offline.html` to `/offline`, and a redirected response cannot be shown for a navigation. The worker now saves and serves `/offline` and never stores redirected responses (found while verifying live; fixed in the follow-up PR).
- Checked in headless Chrome with the server stopped: saved pages open, unsaved ones show the offline page with the links. Not checked on a real phone or iOS.
- Not done: the "You are offline, showing the list from..." banner inside the map (app.js), self-hosting MapLibre, offline tiles.

## Batch D 3: slimmer /api/events (2026-11-06)
`/api/events` now leaves out events that ended more than 3 days ago (`ENDED_GRACE_DAYS` in `functions/api/events.js`) and filters in SQL, so it reads fewer rows. `?ended=1` returns everything (same house de-duplication), `?all=1` stays the admin's full list. Every response has a weak `ETag`; a matching `If-None-Match` gets an empty 304 (also from the edge cache). Event objects are unchanged, and `public/app.js` and `styles.css` were not touched (no `?v=` bump). The app already hid ended events (`ev.e < TODAY`), so the visible result is identical; only the download shrinks. Tests: `tests/events-api.test.mjs`. Not done: a Cache Rule so hits skip the Function (needs the dashboard and a test), a precomputed snapshot, fewer fields.

## Batch D 2: alerting (2026-11-06)
- Failed emails are never marked sent: the weekly alert (`alerts/send.js`) only sets `last_sent` after a successful send and returns `failed`; `alerts.yml` stops with an error if a whole batch failed; contest open/close reminders are only marked done when something went out (an unsubscribed recipient is not a failure).
- `sendMail` no longer throws on a network error; it returns false and logs. New table `ops_log` (migration 0163, no personal data, 30-day prune) and `functions/_lib/oplog.js` (`logOp`, `opsSince`). Logged: failed or unreachable mail service, missing `RESEND_API_KEY`, Stripe webhook handler errors and 5xx answers.
- The admin digest email now starts with "Problems the site noticed" (subject starts "PROBLEMS") when anything was logged since the last digest.
- GitHub issues (emails the owner): uptime check ("Site is down", closes itself on recovery), migrations (validate or apply failed), admin digest not sent, nightly backup. Tests: `tests/alerting.test.mjs`.
- Not done: Workers Logs (`[observability]` in wrangler.toml, untested on Pages); Cloudflare notification policies (owner, dashboard).

Entries are approved, photo-less, `user_id=''`, tagged "Added by organizer". Account deletion keeps them. Privacy page sentence added (updated Nov 5). Tests: `tests/organizer-entries.test.mjs`. ## Audit batch A: six safe fixes (2026-11-04, branch `claude/quirky-goodall-yaumg1`, not merged)
- **Fake "City of X" contests:** contests still go live at once, but one with a review note (`contests.note`, set for free-mail, no website or mismatch) cannot buy Featured until Admin clicks "Looks fine" (the existing Contests tab action; it clears the note and is logged). `/api/me/featurable` shows it as state `review`. The dashboard and tab counts already include live contests with a note.
- **Invite abuse and email injection:** migration 0158 `email_send_log`; `functions/_lib/sendlimit.js`. Neighbor invitations: 10 addresses per call, 30 per contest per day; every third-party email (invites, judges, co-organizers, judge reminders) counts toward 100 per account per day (429). The invite note loses links and is 300 characters. `functions/_lib/mailtpl.js` neutralizes typed values (brackets, parentheses, angle brackets, backticks, web addresses, line breaks) before the layout is built, and `esc` now escapes quotes.
- **Locked results:** after announce, approve, restore, decline, flag and entrant edits return 409 "Results are locked."; the announce write is `WHERE announced = 0` (double click writes once); announcing before voting starts or with no votes needs `force` (409 `needs_force`; organizer page asks "Announce anyway?").
- **Security headers:** `functions/_middleware.js` adds nosniff, Referrer-Policy, Permissions-Policy and X-Frame-Options to Function responses; `public/_headers` has the same for static files. **No Content-Security-Policy yet (deferred: needs a long test across the map, Turnstile, Google sign-in, unpkg, fonts and admin).** `public/_routes.json` lists the paths that run Functions so the new root middleware does not turn every static file into a Function call. **A new top-level Function route must be added there.**
- **Real 404s:** `public/404.html`; unknown paths now return 404 (every real route re-checked, still 200).
- **SEO:** city pages emit Event JSON-LD only for confirmed events, titles are at most 60 characters, and a "More places" block links to other cities in the county plus /add-event and /pricing; the sitemap has no `lastmod` and lists `/past` only when a contest has been announced.
- Tests: `npm test` (mail injection unit tests). Not fixed here: `/c/<unknown>` still returns 200 (ux F1 second half).

## Monetization project (2026-11-04, branch `claude/quirky-goodall-yaumg1`)
Docs in `docs/monetization/` (`strategy.md` v2, `data-2026-11-04.md`, three department reports, `project.md`). Real data overturned the first plan: Stripe shows $0 ever paid, real traffic is about 20-50 human visits a day, only 6 homes and 1 installer exist. Strategy: prove the payment path, fix the Featured webhook (found a bug: failed first attempt is never applied on retry), measure, grow audience and supply free, sell only Featured and affiliate links this season. **Merged and live 2026-11-04 (PR 291):** Featured payment fixes (retry-safe webhook, refund and dispute handling, one price source, receipts, native-app guard, menu link) and migration 0155, verified on the live database and site. Still needed in Stripe (owner): add webhook events `charge.refunded`, `charge.dispute.created`, `charge.dispute.closed`, `checkout.session.expired`; turn on Successful payments receipts; remove `STRIPE_READONLY_KEY` from the Pages secrets. Next: $5 test purchase, outreach (`docs/monetization/outreach.md`), Search Console recheck. Owner decisions are at the end of `strategy.md`. **Also merged 2026-11-04 (PR 296):** privacy-light click counters (7 fixed keys in `site_hits`, shown in Admin dashboard under "Taps on links and buttons"), Featured FAQ note that a refund or dispute switches the placement off. **Held, not shipped:** "Sponsored" labels, privacy-policy and prize wording, installer license text, new refund-window rules (see `legal-redlines.md`, `finance-operations.md`); no design changes while the tutorial videos are being recorded. **Ready for the owner:** researched outreach with 12 send-ready emails (`outreach-targets.md`; best first sends Mission Viejo MVAC, Mom's LA, Enjoy Orange County), CPA brief, affiliate plan (apply to Fever first). Use 1,244 upcoming listings (579 confirmed), not 2,013, in anything public. **Cloudflare setup (2026-11-04):** separate preview environment created (D1 `socal-lights-preview`, R2 `socal-lights-photos-preview`; same 180 migrations, no personal data; Pages preview bindings point at them, production untouched; setup config kept outside the repo). Free uptime check added (`.github/workflows/uptime.yml`, every 15 minutes, GitHub emails on failure). The Android key workflow was deleted because it published the key as an artifact in a public repo; see `docs/android-signing.md` (make a new key before the first Play upload). `MAIL_POSTAL_ADDRESS` is set as a Pages secret (the project's variables are managed by wrangler.toml, so only secrets can be added in the dashboard). Not done: DMARC record and minimum TLS 1.2 (blocked by the session's DNS safety rule; the owner adds them in the dashboard). **Pricing (2026-11-04):** Featured is now $10 / $15 / $20 (pin / top of the list / whole season), set only in `functions/_lib/featured.js`. New public `/pricing` page (free for homeowners; free for now for installers, event organizers and contest organizers; Featured prices read live from `/api/config`; hidden in the native app), linked from the Add hub, every page footer, `/feature` and the sitemap. **Campaign attribution (2026-11-04):** the owner runs Grok outreach to installers and contest organizers; landings with `?ref=inst|cont|pub|soc` are counted in `site_hits` (keys `ref:*`, admin dashboard, same privacy design); fact sheet and guardrails in `docs/monetization/grok-outreach-guide.md`. Privacy policy now mentions the tap counts.

## Shared editing, change logs, cancellations, on-site reports (2026-11-02, migration 0143)
- **Homeowner and installer share a listing.** Once the installer confirms they installed a home, the installer's profile owner can edit it too (only the person who created the listing can take it down or put it back). Every edit is saved as "who changed which field, from what to what" (`listing_changes`) and both people are emailed (including a receipt to whoever made the change; take-downs and put-backs too). Shown on the homeowner's Profile > Displays and the installer's manage page, with a "Last change" line.
- **Contest team change log.** Edits, switches (entries, voting, counts), Start voting, announcing, sponsors, judges and co-organizers are saved in `contest_changes`; the others on the team are emailed, the dashboard shows a banner for the latest change by someone else and a "What the team changed" card.
- **Cancellations in sweeps.** Sweeps set `notice` with an UPDATE (rules in `docs/updating-events.md` rule 3 and `docs/sweep-methods.md`); upserts never touch it.
- **"I saw it lit tonight"** is self-reported. If the visitor allows location and is within about a quarter mile of the home it counts as "seen lit on site"; the card says Reported or Seen on site.

## Data normalization (2026-11-02, migration 0144)
Phones, websites, emails, ZIPs, streets and names are tidied as people type (`public/norm-ui.js`) and again on the server (`public/norm.js`, `functions/_lib/fields.js`); phones show as (310) 555-0123 everywhere and the installer label reads "Call for Quotes". Admin > Data check audits and tidies older records. Event price wording unified (0144). Full notes: `docs/data-audit.md`.

## Where we are
Site is live at https://socallights.app. Event data is being maintained through weekly sweeps (see the latest `migrations/00NN_sweep_*` files and `docs/confirmation-plan.md`). The repository moved from the personal GitHub account to the `Forza-TEK` org on 2026-10-27.

## Open questions / blockers
- **Some event sources block automated visitors.** The cloud environment's Network access is already **Full** (checked 2026-10-31), so no setting helps. Sites that answer 403 (Hawthorne, Santa Monica, West Hollywood, Carlsbad, Inglewood, Eastvale, Perris, Chula Vista, Axios, Macaroni Kid) refuse bots: use second-hand sources (local news, Patch, parks pages) or ask the city by email. These load fine: Riverside County Library `rivlib.libcal.com` (JSON: `/ajax/calendar/list?c=-1&date=YYYY-MM-DD&daily=true&perpage=200&page=1&inc=0` with header `X-Requested-With: XMLHttpRequest`), San Bernardino County Library `library.sbcounty.gov` (public events API: `/wp-json/tribe/events/v1/events?per_page=50&start_date=…&end_date=…&search=holiday`), Culver City, Oceanside, Thousand Oaks and Gardena sites. Earlier notes listing `rivcolibrary.org` and `sblibrary.org` used wrong addresses. A keyword pass over both library calendars for Nov–Dec 2026 found nothing new: Wrightwood Santa visit, Fontana craft fair and the other holiday items were already on the map.
- **Leads not yet published (need a manual check or a reachable source):** Cudahy, Paramount and South Gate Halloween events (2026 dates only estimated from 2025); Leimert Park Kwanzaa parade (Dec 26, no loaded 2025 page); LA County Parks Trick or Treat Village and Harvest Festival (flyer images only); LA City Rec & Parks Halloween Activities List PDF (403); Riverside Bryant Park Halloween carnival (Oct 31, snippet only); Covina Woman's Club trunk-or-treat; Cerritos Artesia Park Spooktacular; Whittier Christmas parade; Bellflower tree lighting; Long Beach Aquarium NYE; HB Central Library Nutcracker shows (no fitting christmas type).

## Recently done
- Amazon Associates tracking ID `socallightsap-20` is set in `public/site-config.js`; the "Shop for the season" links in the mug box are live. Amazon reviews the account after 3 qualifying sales within 180 days. Ticket-affiliate programs (Impact etc.) still to be applied for.
- Added a "Shows & concerts" type (`show`) to the Thanksgiving & Holiday Lights season and published 44 held-back holiday shows, ballets, concerts and the Turkey Night Grand Prix (Thanksgiving-themed car race). Shows that start after Dec 26 sit in the New Year's season (`show`, "Shows & festivals").
- Desktop event/house list now uses the same photo-on-top card layout as the phone (card rules moved out of the phone-only media query in `public/styles.css`, with a hover outline).
- UI: Events/Houses headers now always limit to the map and carry "+ Add event" / "+ Add house" buttons (the "Limit to map" checkbox is gone); the coffee-mug box only has the tip link and shop links; `/add-event` can take a Featured payment (held until approval if the event needs review).
- Added a public "Add your event" form (`/add-event`, auto-publishes when the event page confirms the name and year, otherwise admin review) and automatic Featured payments via Stripe Checkout + webhook (migration 0065, `docs/stripe-setup.md`). **Featured stays on the Cash App/email fallback until `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` and `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` are set as Cloudflare Pages secrets.**
- Posted 7 expected (unconfirmed) events held back earlier: Cudahy, Paramount, South Gate, Leimert Park Kwanzaa, Whittier parade, Bellflower tree lighting, Aquarium NYE.
- Monetization added (see `docs/monetization.md`): support button with Cash App tip link, affiliate-link plumbing (off until Amazon tag / ticket affiliate ids are set in `public/site-config.js`), `/feature.html` plus `featured` flag on events (migration 0062).
- House cards with a photo now drop the badge icon outright (`.thumb.has-photo::before{display:none}`) instead of relying on the photo painting over it; the earlier stacking fix still showed the badge on an iPhone.
- Fixed: the badge icon showed on top of a showcased house's photo on its card; it now only appears for houses without a photo.
- Showcased houses can be starred (heart on the card and in the detail) and are included in the route plan next to events (stored in the same `socal-favs` list as `h:<id>`; the route uses the house's street address). Houses with an address are no longer nudged away from it (the city-level spreading now applies only to older posts without one). Single-stop routes with "Start from: First stop" now have a destination.
- Map: the locate-me button now flies in to street level (zoom 15) on the user's position; taps on empty map deselect reliably (`clickTolerance` raised from 3 to 10 px so a tap with finger wobble still counts as a click, and deselecting also closes an open card detail).
- Admin: every house card (pending and published) has an Edit button. Saving with a street address geocodes it and moves the pin (county recomputed); season, city and the other fields are editable too. Fixes houses submitted before the address field (pin at the city center, no address). The old `edit` API action also used to reset the pin to the city center; it no longer does.
- Showcase a House form collects a required street address (migration 0060 adds `posts.address` and `posts.show_address`; `show_address` is now always set to 1). The server always geocodes it (US Census, then OpenStreetMap; rejects matches over 30 km from the chosen city) and puts the pin there; visitors see the address with a Directions link. If no match is found the pin falls back to the city center and the admin can move it. Not yet tried against the live geocoders (the sandbox can't reach them): submit a real address once and check the pin.
- Night mode is automatic: dark from 6 pm to 6 am (device time), light otherwise. The sun/moon button overrides it until the next 6 am / 6 pm switch, then auto resumes; a page left open flips on its own.
- Cache: Cloudflare gives CSS/JS a 4-hour browser cache that `_headers` can't override (the zone's Browser Cache TTL), so phones kept the old UI after deploys. `public/index.html` now loads `styles.css` and `app.js` with a `?v=` version; bump it whenever either file changes (rule added to `CLAUDE.md`). A zone-level fix would be to set Browser Cache TTL to "Respect Existing Headers" in the Cloudflare dashboard.
- Phone UI, all live on `main` (PRs #19-#21): the list uses one card format and is always vertical (sideways card row and the "cards" sheet stop removed); season tabs have no underline, and a dot in the season's color marks the selected tab; Holidays has a caret drop-down listing its event types plus "All Holidays"; the selected card stays visible when the sheet moves or after "Back to results".
- Phone list: the half and full-height list now uses the same cards as the collapsed sheet (84px image, dates and tags shown in both).
- 2026-10-28 library/park research push, all merged to `main` and applied: Huntington Beach Central Library (2), South Bay (8), Gateway Cities (5), Orange County libraries (11), San Diego/Ventura/Santa Barbara libraries (24), Long Beach and Southeast LA (15, plus a Santa Fe Springs tree lighting date fix to Nov 21), LA County/Glendale/Burbank/LAPL libraries (20), Inglewood/Gardena/Lawndale (3). Live feed went from 1,019 to roughly 1,100 events. `docs/city-sweep.md` now requires searching library and park calendars.
- Scheduled routines publish directly to `main` after validation; weekly upkeep and city sweep test runs both landed migrations successfully.
- City sweep group 21 (region-wide sources), 2026-10-27 evening: regional guides (enjoyorangecounty, lajollamom, sandiego.org, Visit Newport Beach, Secret LA, mommypoppins) are almost fully covered on the map already; added 1 event (Fairmont Grand Del Mar tree lighting, migration 0049). Left out for unverified 2026 dates: Park Hyatt Aviara Stocking Full of Christmas, Night of Lights OC, San Diego Harvest Festival at Dolores Magdaleno Rec Center (Nov 21, cost not listed).
- Coverage audit: all 210 incorporated cities checked against `docs/coverage-cities.md`; 13 missing cities added to groups and a priority list of 0–1-event cities added (see that file).
- Recreated the three scheduled routines against `Forza-TEK/socal-lights-app` and disabled the old ones (2026-10-28). Test-fired the city sweep; it cloned the repo and started searching.
- Verified `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN` survived the transfer: a manual run of the migrations workflow on 2026-10-27 authenticated and applied cleanly to the live D1 database (no-op, everything already applied). Note the workflow skips itself and still shows green if the secret is missing, so check that the "Apply migrations" step actually ran.
