## Showcase a House form redesign (2026-10-29)
- The form has a switch at the top: **A homeowner** or **A professional installer**. Website, phone, service area and the "permission to show this address" checkbox appear only for installers (they need a website or phone). Homeowners enter just their name, email and the display.
- The address is now separate Street, City, State (CA) and ZIP fields. `posts.address` still holds the full one-line address (pin, directions and routes use it), built as "street, city, CA zip".
- Migration `0076_showcase_address_fields.sql` adds `poster_type`, `street`, `state`, `zip`, `service_area` and carries the 4 existing houses over, using the city typed in the address (Mission Viejo became Ladera Ranch, Los Angeles became El Segundo). "Holiday Christmas Lighting" (Lights Up Today) is marked as an installer. Ladera Ranch and El Segundo were added to `public/cities.js`.
- House pages show a "Professional installer" tag and service area for installers, and "Posted by" for homeowners. The admin edit dialog has the same fields.

## UI redesign (2026-10-29)
- **Top bar:** search, one **Season** drop-down (Halloween ▾; under the search on phones) and a **⋯ menu** (Support SoCal Lights, Night mode switch, Add an event, Showcase a house, Contact). The donate and night-mode icons, the season bar and the row of filter chips are gone from the map.
- **Filters:** one **Filters** button in the list header opens a panel (When, Near, County, Cost, Type, Confirmed dates only, Show on map) with a live "Show N events" button; active filters show as removable chips. Tonight / Saturday / Free stay as shortcuts. New **Sort** menu (Soonest, Free first, Nearest).
- **List:** Redfin-style cards (photo, price line, dates and hours, name, place, one-line description, heart and share). The right-hand panel is wider on large screens (540 px at 1100 px+, 700 px at 1360 px+) and shows two columns. A "Keep it free" line ends the events list.
- **Tap a dot:** a card opens next to the dot on desktop (list stays on the right) and docks at the bottom on phones, with heart, share and ×. Esc or an empty-map tap closes it.
- **Phone list:** two positions only, Peek (handle and the Filters / Tonight / Saturday / Free buttons) and Full; the handle toggles them.
- **Share links:** `#e-<event id>` and `#h-<house id>` open an event or house directly.
- List cards now use the same layout as the pin card: photo with date badge, then name with share and heart beside it, place, dates and hours, and the price and Confirmed tags (houses too).
- The ⋯ menu now holds only Support SoCal Lights, Night mode and Contact; Add event and Add house stay in the list header.
- The "Coming up" banner now sits at the end of the events list, above "Enjoying SoCal Lights? Keep it free".
- The ⋯ menu's support item is now "Keep SoCal Lights Free" with the hand-and-heart icon.
- Removed the colored dot from the Season button.
- Phone list sheet: one full-height panel that only slides (a GPU transform), so the buttons, the count / sort / + Add line and the cards never move relative to the sheet and nothing is rebuilt while it moves. Peek shows the handle and the Filters / Tonight / Saturday / Free row; a soft fade behind the tab bar hides the rest of the sheet at Peek and fades out as the sheet rises, so the list shows behind the tab bar at Full. The first 8 event photos load eagerly.

## One list: Places (2026-10-29)
- The Events and Houses tabs are now one **Places** tab (map-pin icon): events and home displays share one list and one map. Tabs are Places, My night, Shop.
- Home displays show a blue **Home display** badge where events show a date, "Posted by …", "<Season> display · Free to view", and tags. Same card layout, heart and share.
- Filters: dates, Confirmed and Ticketed only narrow events (home displays have no dates or prices; they count as Free). Type has a new **Home displays** chip. The "Show on map" switches are gone.
- Sort: **Nearest** is the default (distance from the map center), then Soonest and Free first. Paid "top of the list" placements stay first in every order.
- "+ Add event" and "+ Add house" became one **+ Add ▾** button with Add an event and Showcase your house. `#houses` links no longer need a tab; `#h-<id>` and `#showcase` still work.
- **My night** now uses the same layout as Places: the Open route / Start / Maps controls sit in the buttons row, the count line shows stops, miles and drive time, and the cards (with a stop number and "↓ N mi · ~N min from stop N") fill the same list. The old Open route button block, "Open in" picker and notes were tidied into the controls row and a short notes list under the cards.
- Motion: the active tab grows its label while the old one folds away, tab content slides in, and menus, the season list and the Filters panel spring open with their rows sliding in one by one (all off for reduced-motion).
- Phone, night mode only: the list sheet is slightly transparent with a soft blur. Count badges on the tabs have more space from their labels.
- Pin card: a home display with more than one photo can be swiped through (arrows on desktop hover, a 1 / N counter); swiping does not open the details.
- Close buttons use a drawn X so it sits dead center in its circle. The pin card's photo arrows are always visible on desktop, with dots, a counter and the left/right arrow keys.
- Pin card with several photos: the photo area only browses (arrows, clickable dots, swipe, arrow keys); tapping the text below opens the details.
- My night help: three simple steps (tap the heart, check your stops, tap Open route) that fold into a ? button beside the stop count; the choice is remembered on the device.
- Fixed: the soft fade behind the phone tab bar was covering the lower half of the pin card (a merged CSS rule had lost its hide-when-card-open selector).
- Phone details page: the back arrow stays fixed at the top of the sheet while the details scroll.
- Season button: a larger chevron that flips when the list is open; lighter type on the season button, menus and buttons.
- Home display details use the same round back arrow as events (over the photo). Menu, button and filter type is one step lighter.
- Home display details have the same orange Directions button as events (to the street address).
- The Shop tab's season drop-down has a clear chevron sized to its text, like the main season button.
- The Holidays season is now called Christmas in the season list, the Shop tab and filters and its full name is Thanksgiving & Christmas Lights.
- 2026-10-29 city sweep group 4 (Pomona Valley & East SGV): added Claremont Turkey Trot, Irwindale Christmas Spectacular (expected), West Covina Children's Christmas Parade (migrations 0077, 0078). Glendora Holiday Stroll and Azusa not added: no official 2026 page found.
- Menu icons (plus, house, moon, mail) are drawn icons, centered in their circles.
- Phone: after opening a place's details and tapping back, the list keeps that place listed (the map was being recentered under the open sheet, which pushed it out of view and could leave an empty list).

## Installable app (PWA) (2026-10-29)
- SoCal Lights can now be installed: `public/manifest.webmanifest`, icons in `public/img/` (192, 512 and a maskable 512), `public/sw.js` (network first, offline fallback page `public/offline.html`) and iOS home-screen meta tags in `index.html`. There is deliberately no "Get the app" link in the site yet; add one only after the store apps exist.
- App shortcuts: My night (`/#night`) and Add an event.
- Next, if wanted: Google Play (Trusted Web Activity, $25), Microsoft Store (PWABuilder, $19), Apple App Store (Capacitor, $99/yr; needs a Mac and some native features). See the plan in the chat of 2026-10-29.
- My night: fixed the steps box (the done step picked up the success-message styling and sat off-center; it now shows a check) and gave the route notes a clean info style.
- My night: unfavoriting the last stop returns to Places with the whole map in view.
- On phones, that return to Places also lowers the list to Peek so the map shows.

## 2026-10-31 — Session wrap-up: sweep reports, photo gaps, app changes
**Sweep review.** Migrations from the 21 fired city sweeps (groups 1, 3, 5, 7, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 18, 19, 21 plus High Desert, Eastvale and western Riverside runs) are all on `main` and every event id from them is live (1,958 events on the API). Six `Apply database migrations` runs were cancelled when pushes landed close together; the next run applied everything (D1 tracks applied files), and the latest runs are green. Groups 2, 6, 8, 9, 10, 17 and 20 left no 2026-10-30 report or migration (nothing to add, or the run did not finish): re-run any of them with "Run GROUP N" if you want proof.
- **Recurring blocker:** city sites (Hawthorne, Duarte, Monrovia, Eastvale, Norco, Jurupa Valley, Perris, Santa Monica, WeHo, Culver City, Axios, Macaroni Kid) return 403 or fail DNS from the cloud sandbox. Widen Network access for the session and routine environments (see Open questions).
- **Pins to re-check:** Cathedral City Día de Muertos (31705 Da Vall Dr), Desert Highland carnival (~0.8 km), Indio Tamale Festival (~0.9 km).
- **Leads not added** (no 2026 date or address on a loaded page): see each group's lines above; December contest lists (Glendora, Covina, Lakewood, Signal Hill, Paramount, Santa Maria, Beaumont, Alhambra, Monrovia, Temple City) are tracked in `docs/contest-leads.md`. Burbank is skipped on purpose.

**Photo gaps.** 862 live events still have no photo. 365 sit on a page shared by more than 3 events (enjoyorangecounty, sceneoc, Temecula's display list, Menifee, Murrieta); 497 have their own page. A fourth `scripts/photo_pass.py` run over 492 pages found only 15 candidates and 14 were logos, a black frame, a landscape or one picture shared by unrelated homes, so only the Chino youth parade photo was kept (migration 0114). The 23 Mission Viejo honorable-mention homes have no photos on mvactivities.com (the page only shows the winners; the holiday PDF has tiny unlabeled thumbnails). What remains needs a person: ask homeowners, or use photos submitted through "Add a house".

**App changes this session (all live).** Photo galleries with arrows and a counter; full-size photo viewer (lightbox); thin icons and dark controls on cards; color day map and a night map made by dimming it (DIM 0.60); faster popup cards; night-mode switch in the ⋯ menu; desktop hide/show list handle; Shop expanded to 10–13 categories per season with 12 items each, sorted high to low price, desktop tabs wrap and phone tabs nudge once; thinner button type in the list, My night and Shop; Filters > Type has Contests; "Route these" link next to the place count (zoomed in past the number circles and 9 or fewer places, greyed with a hint otherwise); add page header no longer has Back to the map. Pin audit round 2 moved 9 pins (migration 0112). Latest asset version `20261031j`.

- 2026-10-31 add forms: the event web page is optional ("Do you have a web page for your event?" with a "No... Next" link; without a page the event waits for a person to review it). Save & exit sits left of the day/night toggle, and a signed-in circle (picture or initial, opens the profile) shows on the forms and hub. Leaving a form with unsaved changes (header links, Back on the first step, closing the tab) offers to save the draft first.

- 2026-10-31 admin restyled to the forms theme (`public/admin/admin.css`: black and white, light type, hairline borders, season-color tab underline, logo, light/dark toggle); every page now has a favicon (admin and offline pages added, plus `/favicon.ico`).

- 2026-10-31 contest page, organizer dashboard restyled to the forms look (`public/contest.css` on top of `public/addflow.css`): logo header, day/night toggle, signed-in circle, hairline cards, outlined pills, black buttons. Behavior unchanged.

- 2026-10-31 profile tabs (Drafts, Contests, Entries, Displays, Events, Account) with optional name and address on the Account tab (migration 0115: `users.street/city/zip`, `/api/me/profile`); forms start with the saved name and address when signed in. One `attachAddress()` helper (`public/addflow.js`) gives every address field the suggestion dropdown: the add forms, contest entry, the profile, and the admin edit windows (house street, event address).

- 2026-10-31 leftover screens restyled (privacy, feature, offline now in the forms look via `public/info.css`); self-serve account deletion (`DELETE /api/me`, Profile, Account tab; privacy page updated); in the native apps the sign-in sheet offers the emailed code only (Google blocks embedded browsers, so Google is hidden when the user agent is `SoCalLightsApp`); store promo text now says free to browse, sign in only to enter a contest.

- 2026-10-31 judges, prizes and archive (migration 0116): contests can be public, judges only, or both (People's Choice and Judges' Choice, separate awards); judges are invited by email, sign in with that email at `/c/<slug>/judge`, and either pick one home per category or score every home 1-10 (trimmed mean at 5+ judges, conflicts recuse, announce warns if a judge hasn't finished). Prizes: none, one for all, or one per award, plus a sponsor line; shown on the contest page, in winners and in emails. `/archive` lists finished contests by Season | Year from the frozen snapshot (link in the map menu). Judges can't vote publicly. Winner badges on listings are next (same migration adds `users.badges`).

- 2026-10-31 winner badges: `/api/posts` adds `b` (badges with list/popup/detail flags after the owner's choices) to homes owned by past winners; `/api/me/badges` (GET/POST) and a profile Badges tab with a preview; one yellow crown icon on the list card (top-right chip), the map pop-up and the details page. On by default.

- 2026-10-31 the archive is now "Contest archives" (`/archive`, map menu): each finished contest lists its winners and every contestant with their votes, judges' picks or scores and awards won, all from the frozen results (`/api/archive` returns `entries`).

## Person's own record replaces the sweep record (branch, not live yet)
- An approved house/installer post at the same street address as a sweep event hides that event (matched in `functions/_lib/addrkey.js`; hidden, never deleted; declining the post restores it). Same street, different number: both stay. See `docs/superseding.md`.
- Admin: note on pending houses, **Replaced records** tab with a "show anyway" override (`events.keep_listed`, migration `0128`), counts on every tab (`/api/admin/counts`), tabs wrap on narrow screens.
- Tested locally: exact match hides after approval (not before), near match stays, override, decline restores, log entries, counts.
- Not done: the Add form does not warn the person at submit time that a sweep record exists at their address (the admin sees it at review).

## Choose which record visitors see (branch)
- On the Replaced records tab, each address has a three-way choice: their record (default), both, or the sweep record. Choosing the sweep record hides the person's post from `/api/posts` (still approved; flagged "Not on the map" in Published houses). `events.keep_listed` 0/1/2, no new migration. Tested locally; default behaviour unchanged.

## Search finds street addresses (branch)
- The map search now matches a street address: homes whose address is public (`address` in `/api/posts`) and every event's address (`a`), with street words shortened on both sides, so "22 Tuberose Street" finds "22 Tuberose St, Ladera Ranch". Homes whose owner chose not to show their address are still not searchable by address. `public/app.js` (`hay`), `?v=20261031u` in `index.html`.

- 2026-10-31 distance on cards (`20261031v`): list cards, map popup card and details show "· 3.2 mi away" (straight-line, from the user's own location). Nothing is asked up front: if location is already allowed for the site it is read quietly; otherwise it starts when the locate button (or My night) is tapped. Browser pass reminders (phone + email) are scheduled for Nov 16, Dec 1 and Dec 14 10:07am PT. Emailed PKS@cityofwhittier.org on 2026-10-31 asking for an events feed or bot allow-listing.

## Contest wins carry over to the replacing record (branch)
- A contest win stated on a replaced sweep record now shows as a crown line on the person's listing (card chip, pop-up, details with Source link); display only, person's text untouched. Rule and limits in `docs/superseding.md`. Tested locally for all three choices. Nothing for wins stated only in the title and not recognised beyond the "(... contest winner)" pattern.

## Home addresses are always public (branch)
- `/api/posts` now always returns a home's street address (`rowToPost` ignores the old `show_address` flag), so address search finds every home and every pin sits on its street address. New posts were already public (`show_address = 1`); the admin edit window already saved them as public. Checked live before the change: all 4 published homes already had a public address. The admin card now says "(shown on the map)" instead of public/private. No migration.

## Save & exit on the contest entry form (branch)
- `/c/<slug>/enter` now has Save & exit, the leave-page prompt and the keep-your-place draft like the other forms. The draft is saved to the account as kind `entry` (photos are not saved) and shows on the profile Drafts tab as "Contest entry", with Continue going back to that contest's form on the step they left (`drafts.data.contestSlug`, listed by `/api/drafts` and `/api/me/overview` as `slug`). Same-device draft also kept per contest (`scl-entry-draft-<slug>`). `addflow.js` bumped to `?v=20261031u` on every page. Tested locally: save, profile row, Continue restores the answers and step. No migration.

Easter's window (Mar 15 – Apr 25) is wide because the date moves. Version `20261101e`.
- 2026-11-01 audit batch 3 (`20261101g/h`): `/api/events` kept at the edge for 2 minutes (not for `?all=1`); search suggestions (cities move the map, places open their details; `showSuggestions` in `public/app.js`); SEO: `public/robots.txt`, `/sitemap.xml` (`functions/sitemap.xml.js`, one entry per city with upcoming events), city pages `/in/<city>` with schema.org Event data (`functions/in/[city].js`), canonical link and WebSite data on the home page. City pages are not linked from the app yet; they are found through the sitemap. After deploy, submit `https://socallights.app/sitemap.xml` in Google Search Console and Bing Webmaster Tools (owner step).
- 2026-11-01 audit: details show "Updated <date>" (`lc` field from `events.updated_at` in `/api/events`); the service worker (`scl-2`) keeps the last `/api/events` and `/api/posts` so the list opens offline (map tiles and photos are not kept); first-visit location offer, share-my-night (`#night-<ids>`), "More in <city>" links, Shop season drop-down kept (defaults to the season button). Version `20261101l`.
- 2026-11-01 weekly lights email (migration 0134 `alert_subs`): menu "Weekly lights email" asks for email, city and distance (5/10/25 mi), Turnstile + consent tick; `POST /api/alerts` sends a confirmation link (`/api/alerts/confirm`), `/api/alerts/unsubscribe` deletes the row; `POST /api/alerts/send` (header `x-alert-key` = `ALERT_CRON_KEY`) emails up to 20 confirmed people per call who have events within their distance in the next 14 days, at most once per 6 days. `.github/workflows/alerts.yml` calls it Thursdays 17:30 UTC and loops until `remaining` is 0. Needs `ALERT_CRON_KEY` as BOTH a Cloudflare Pages secret and a GitHub repository secret (same value); optional Pages variable `ALERT_FOOTER` (postal address line for the email footer). Sends use the existing `RESEND_API_KEY` / `MAIL_FROM`. Resend free plan: 100 emails a day.
- 2026-11-01 photo thumbnails: `photoImg` asks Cloudflare image resizing (`/cdn-cgi/image/width=…`) for smaller copies once a test request succeeds (remembered in localStorage `socal-thumbs`), and falls back to the original photo if a copy fails. Needs Images > Transformations switched on for socallights.app in the Cloudflare dashboard (Images & Stream subscription activated 2026-11-01, $0 base, 5,000 unique transformations a month included, then $1 per 1,000).
- 2026-11-01 weekly email switched on: `ALERT_CRON_KEY` is set as a Pages secret (production) and, per the owner, as a GitHub secret. `ALERT_FOOTER` (mailing address line) was not present in the Pages variables when checked; add it before sending to the public.
- 2026-11-01 weekly email test: the first GitHub run was stopped by a Cloudflare "Just a moment" bot check on socallights.app. The workflow now calls `https://socal-lights.pages.dev/api/alerts/send` (same project, no bot check) and the email links are fixed to `https://socallights.app`.
- 2026-11-01 tab bars: one dark pill glides to the selected tab (`initSlidePill` in `public/app.js`, `.slidepill` in `styles.css`) for the bottom bar on phones and the top tabs on desktop, with no bounce; reduced-motion users get an instant move.

Quote-lead checkboxes name the installer and (contest page) the organizer and list name, email, phone and note. `deleteAccount` now also clears judges, quote requests, edit logs, claims, alert signups, staff rows, report contacts and installer owner emails. Marketing emails (weekly alert, reminders, results, invitations including judges and co-organizers) carry the postal address (read from the `MAIL_POSTAL_ADDRESS` secret, never in the repo) and `List-Unsubscribe` one-click headers; `/api/unsub` and `/api/alerts/unsubscribe` accept the one-click POST. `docs/app-store.md` location answers fixed. Tests: `tests/privacy.test.mjs` (uses `tests/_d1.mjs`, an in-memory D1 stand-in with every migration applied). Left for the attorney: Terms additions, installer licensing text, DMCA.

They now span the full row, inputs fill the width and the receipt picker is a full-width dashed box.

When signed in, Add, Contests, Tutorials and Help leave the three-dot menu (it is shorter); signed-out visitors still see them there. chrome.js/appshell.js/addflow.js versions bumped to 20261110a/c.

- "Keep SoCal Lights Free" popup and menu row reworded to "Support SoCal Lights": says what is free, what paid options fund the site (Featured events, installer banners, extra contest passes), tips welcome, and links to /pricing.

- Map header fixes: signed-out tap on the profile circle opens /profile again (the sign-in sheet has no styles on the map, so it looked like nothing happened); the avatar is no longer squeezed into an oval (button padding); the logo circle is a true circle between 701 and 1499px wide (it was a pill with the logo pushed left).

- Sponsor invite form (/s/<token>): the Message box shows suggested wording in light grey (a placeholder, naming the organizer) so sponsors can tell the organizer they paid; it is not sent unless they type their own.

- Contest page sponsors: logos are bigger on desktop (96px tall, up to 260px wide; presenting sponsors 128px). Test helper addUser now gives test sessions a 10-year expiry: tests that freeze the clock at Nov 6 failed once the real clock passed Nov 7 20:00 UTC (the 1-day test session had expired).

- Day mode: the page background is now white (was warm off-white #f6f4f0) on the map and every page, like the admin page. Dark mode unchanged. Browser theme-colour meta updated to match. Asset ?v= values bumped (styles.css, addflow.css, chrome.js, addflow.js, appshell.js, app.js).

- Emails: the page behind the email card is white (was cream) and the list and code boxes are a neutral light grey, to match the white day-mode app. All 56 emails rendered and checked at 375px and 700px wide: no sideways overflow, no stray placeholders.

- "Ask SoCal Lights" button: on phones (600px and narrower) it is only the round question-mark button (48px); the words stay for screen readers. Desktop unchanged.

- AI assistant: the chat now follows the visitor between pages (kept in the browser tab only; widget loaded on content pages via footer.js); weekend/tonight/tomorrow/this-week questions use real dates; the assistant no longer names its internal blocks; new knowledge chunk on opening the contest dashboard. Privacy wording updated. Handoff for a tuning session: docs/ai-assistant-training-handoff.md.

## 2026-11-16 shrink tutorial videos for cellular
- Re-encoded 75 of the 76 tutorial mp4s over 4 MB (libx264 CRF 27, preset slow, yuv420p, faststart; AAC 64k). Desktop clips capped at 1280x720 (were 2560x1440), phone clips 720 wide (were 1080). Filenames, durations (within 0.1 s) and captions unchanged.
- Total mp4 size 686 MB -> 265 MB. find-map.mp4 (4.6 MB) was left as is because the re-encode was larger.
- Largest remaining: run-custom-mobile 9.0 MB, run-custom 7.9 MB, run-quick-mobile 6.2 MB (all 5 to 7 minute clips); the rest are under 6 MB.
- Recipe for new clips: `ffmpeg -i in.mp4 -vf "scale='min(1280,iw)':-2" -c:v libx264 -preset slow -crf 27 -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:a aac -b:a 64k -movflags +faststart out.mp4` (use 720 wide for phone clips).

## 2026-11-22 (duplicate): Holidays in the Village merged (migration 0285)
- Two rows: the sweep `thousand-oaks-holidays-in-the-village` (pin 34.1836,-118.8877 = The Oaks, 350 W Hillcrest Dr, correct) and the organizer's `u-holidays-in-the-village--4afd74` (typed "350 Hillcrest"; the Census geocoder returns 350 E and 350 W Hillcrest Dr and the form took the first, E, a pin 1.6 km east).
- 0285: the sweep row stays and takes the organizer's 4 photos and official site; the organizer's submission now points at it (address 350 W Hillcrest Dr); the duplicate row is deleted.
- Guard added: `geocodeAddress` reports `info.ambiguous` when the Census returns two matches more than 0.4 km apart; `submit-event` adds "the address matches more than one place (...): check the pin" to the submission note for Admin. It still publishes with the first match.
- Fix to the merge: 0285 deleted the organizer's row without moving its Santa video (an uploaded clip, `ev/u-holidays-in-the-village--4afd74/1795347712908.mov`, still in the photo bucket). 0286 attaches it to the surviving row (approved); `/api/photo/ev/...` now finds a clip by its own key (`WHERE video_file = ?`) instead of by the event id inside the key, so merged listings keep their clips. Lesson: before deleting an event row in a merge, copy video_file/video_ok too (and check every media column).
- Fix: tapping play, pause or full screen on the video in the map's small card opened the details (the card's catch-all click), so the playing video vanished; in the details the same tap opened the photo viewer. Taps on the video slide (`.vs`) now stay on the video in both places. Verified in a browser (play in card stays in the card; maximize via the card text opens the details with the video as the first slide).
- Fix: going full screen on a playing video resizes the window, and the resize handler rebuilt the whole panel (`renderPanel`), which destroyed the video and showed the poster again. Found from Rey's screen recording. The resize and phone/desktop-switch handlers now skip the rebuild while a video is live (`vidLive`). The same rebuild also reset the old separate Watch button, so this was not new with the gallery change, only easier to hit. Verified with a browser resize test.

## 2026-11-23: the details action bar sat too high on iPhone
- The heart/share/calendar/website/Directions bar is `position: sticky`, and a sticky bar is placed from the scroller's padding edge (measured in Chromium: gap = bottom offset + the scroller's bottom padding). On an iPhone that was (safe area 34 + 16 padding) + (34 + 12) = about 96 pt above the screen bottom. Detail mode now has no bottom padding on `.pbody` (an `::after` spacer gives the end-of-list room) and the bar sits at `max(10px, safe-area - 8px)`, the same height as the floating tab bar. Verified the end-of-list clearance in a browser; the iPhone safe area itself can't be emulated here, so the iPhone result is by measurement of the rule, not a device test.
- Admin, phone: the bottom bar now scrolls sideways and holds every section (23 plus a "More" button pinned at the right that still opens the full menu). The open section scrolls into view when you switch. Items are static markup in `public/admin/index.html` (generated from the side menu: if you add a section to the side menu, add it to the bar too); CSS in `admin.css`, scroll-into-view in `admin.js` (setTab).
- Admin phone bar: the "More" button is gone (the bar already holds every section, so the sheet only duplicated it). The last item is now "Site" (View site), the one thing only the sheet had. The phone sheet code was removed from admin.js.
- Fix: the Admin phone bar showed a red dot for every item that has a count, including the plain totals (Published houses, Events, Installers, Users), so dots appeared with nothing to do. Now only the to-do counts get a dot (Houses waiting, Submitted, Claims, Reports, Replaced, Contests, Orders, Grok), the same ones the side menu marks as "act". On production today all of those are 0, except possibly Replaced, which I did not query.

## 2026-11-24: add-event offers matching listings; choosing one updates it (no claim step)
- New `functions/_lib/eventmatch.js` + `GET /api/events/match` (signed in): listings on overlapping dates within 30 km of the chosen city with a similar name (generic words like "holiday", "lights", "tour" ignored, at least 2 shared words or all of a short name) or the same house number + street (direction and street type ignored). Each comes back with `mine` / `managed` (an organizer or an approved claim already owns it; never named).
- Add-event form: after "Where is it?" a step "Is your event already on the map?" appears only when something matches (`addflow.js` got an async `leave` step hook). Pick a listing, or "No, mine is a different event". The review screen says "Updates the one already on the map".
- `POST /api/submit-event` with `match_id`: only an id the matcher returns for what was entered; updates THAT listing with the organizer's details (their page, if given, else the old one stays; the sweep's picture and any video stay), adds a published `event_submissions` row so it is theirs to manage (this replaces the claim step), and writes what was there into the submission's note (visible in Admin > Submitted events). A listing someone else manages: 409, nothing changed. Already theirs: the "already added" screen.
- Still open: a later sweep migration can overwrite an organizer's edits to a sweep row (no protection yet; the validator only guards Admin-deleted ids).
- Tested with the real wizard in a browser up to the review screen; the final Add press could not be exercised here because the Turnstile widget cannot load offline (the submit path is covered by tests/event-match.test.mjs).
- Protection added: the migration gate now refuses a new migration that names an organizer-managed event (a published submission or an approved claim points at it). `d1-migrations.yml` exports the ids from the live database (`--owned-file`); override with `-- validate: overwrite-owned <id>` (a verified cancellation, an Admin removal). Today 9 events are listed (including `trolley` and `thousand-oaks-holidays-in-the-village`). Documented for the sweeps in `docs/updating-events.md`. It is a text check on the ids in the new migration files; it cannot stop an Admin or the app from editing an event, only migrations.

## 2026-11-24: two audits
- `docs/audit-2026-11-24-functions.md` (50 findings: 2 P0, 11 P1, 23 P2, 14 P3) and `docs/audit-2026-11-24-ui.md` (29 findings: 1 P0, 9 P1, 9 P2, 10 P3; 341 screenshots in the session scratchpad, not in the repo). Spot-checked by hand: 372 of 1,325 live events still "expected" (219 start within 14 days), the App Store review note wrongly says listings are reviewed before publishing, and the details view is wider than a 360/390 phone (grid column sized to its content: `.detail` needs `grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr)`).
- Nothing from the audits is fixed yet. Next: the two P0s of each audit, then the P1s.

## 2026-11-24: Admin > Users shows what each person bought
- `functions/_lib/userbuys.js`: a person's Featured orders (events, homes, installer placements, contest passes, Event Partner plans), matched by account id or by the email they paid with, plus the free contest passes Admin granted to organizations whose contests they run. `GET /api/admin/users/:id` returns it as `purchases`; the Users list gets `buys` and `spent` (paid, held and dispute-won orders; refunded ones are listed but not counted).
- Admin UI: a Purchases column in the Users list ($ paid and order count, opens the person at their Purchases section), and a Purchases section in the person's card (what, plan, amount, date, status, and the total). The CSV gets `purchases` and `paid_dollars`. The event editor already had its own Purchases section; home and contest editors do not yet.

## 2026-11-24: photos missing in the "More events in <city>" list
- The shared upcoming-events list (`_lib/upcoming.js`, used by every /e/<id> page) read only the linked `image`, so an event whose photo was uploaded by an organizer or Admin (`owner_photos`, e.g. Harbor Glow) showed a blank box. It now uses the organizer's photo first, like the map card and the event page. Events with no photo at all (3 of the 6 in the screenshot: Chatsworth brunch, Calvary Chapel, Lake Balboa) now show a letter tile instead of an empty gray box; the city pages (/in/<city>) get the same tile. Those three still need a real photo: they are Grok rows with none (Admin > Events > Edit > Photos).

## 2026-11-24: task list batch (owner answered the task list; "Proceed")
Answers: do T1, T5-T16, T18-T21, T24-T26; skip T2, T3, T4, T17, T23; later T22. Done here (the rest are in parallel branches, merged separately):
- T1: the App Store review note and the 2026-11-16 policy doc no longer say a person reviews every listing first; they now say what the code does (signed in + human check, automatic checks hold some, Report and Hide everywhere, reports reviewed).
- T9: `timeout-minutes` on every workflow job (it was missing on 7 workflows, including Tests and d1-migrations); a post-migration smoke check in `d1-migrations.yml` (event count within 5% of before, `/api/events?head=1` answers 200; a failure opens the existing migration-failed issue). Date-dependent tests: found a real bug, `isDate` accepted "2026-13-45" (only an accident of string comparison rejected it until the calendar moved); `isDate` now requires a real calendar day. The headOf test now uses the season in force today. Not changed: the verify-events test mixes the JS clock with SQL `date('now')` (a harness artifact, not a product bug), and a nightly shifted-clock CI job is not added until the harness handles SQL time.
- T10: the inline-free CSP directives are enforced (`frame-ancestors 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'`, runbook step 3; `form-action` stays report-only); a small error beacon (`public/errors.js` -> `POST /api/client-error` -> Admin activity log, at most 3 per page load, 20 an hour per connection, the same line once an hour, no personal data). Not done: setting `TURNSTILE_REQUIRED=1` in production (it only matters if the Turnstile secret were lost; setting it without the secret present would block every submission, and I cannot read the secrets): set it in Cloudflare Pages > Settings > Variables once you have confirmed `TURNSTILE_SECRET` is there.
- T24: the Santa clip is H.264 + AAC inside a QuickTime wrapper; remuxed (no re-encode) to MP4 with the index at the front. Uploading it to the live bucket was refused (the Cloudflare token I have cannot write R2), so it goes in through Admin > Events > Holidays in the Village > Edit > Video.
